## Goals
1. Add **POS Inventory** option to **Faisal Profile** (view-only access) with POS Inventory nav item.
2. Add **Variant Search** inside each product's variant list (per-product, filtering by color/size/barcode).
3. Display **Warehouse stock** column alongside outlet stocks (JT/JR/AB/WH) in unified inventory view.
4. Implement **POS Open Book / Close Book** workflow for daily cashier shift management and end-of-day reconciliation.
5. Implement **Inventory Audit** module (Warehouse + Outlet): read-only snapshot on start, barcode scanning with live progress, Admin-only approve/reject, automatic inventory updates + adjustment logs on approval.

## Constraints & Preferences
- QR code on receipt must encode per-outlet Google Maps review URL (not receipt data)
- QR must print at the very bottom of the receipt (after all text)
- Receipt must show column headings: ITEM, QTYÃ—PRICE, TOTAL
- Audit (in-progress or submitted) must never touch live inventory â€” sales, transfers, production, dispatch, and scanning continue unaffected
- Inventory updates happen only on Admin approval of a submitted audit, automatically, without manual stock entry
- Approval must also auto-create inventory adjustment log records (previous/new qty, difference, approved by)
- Barcode integrity: never regenerate, modify, or replace barcodes/SKUs/variant IDs/product IDs during an audit â€” only quantities change
- Warehouse variant barcodes are non-persisted computed `WRH...` strings â€” the audit resolves them with the same djb2/generateBarcode algorithm as `warehouse.controller.js` so scanned codes match
- Order number auto-generated when order-number search finds no client; falls back to manual input only if generation fails
- Inventory data must never be deleted or modified by any cleanup script
- Cart panel must scroll fully so products + summary are both visible
- Balance orders (advance + POS) must be tracked in dashboard with ORDER badge
- Print options modal must let user pick Complete Invoice / Gate Pass (or both) before printing
- Client Registration measurements use flat key-value; Outlet Order Entry uses per-product nested structure
- Backend must NOT write per-product sizeData back to Client.sizeDetails (corrupts flat format)
- All changes must preserve existing Online/Faisal/Admin workflows â€” Outlet-only modifications
- POS Dashboard and Total Invoices must be accessible from Outlet Dashboard without modifying existing POS billing workflow
- Do not modify Admin Dashboard, Faisal/Online Dashboard, or existing reports/analytics/APIs
- Balance status must be computed from actual payment records: remaining = grandTotal âˆ’ (advanceAmount + sum of all balancePayments)
- Only invoices with outstanding balance > 0.01 show BAL status; fully paid invoices show PAID
- Filters (All / Paid / Balance) must send statusFilter to backend; backend filters before returning

## Progress
### Done
- **Replacement duplicate guards re-keyed to the live REP instance** (commit `86ecbe4`): Faisal-profile replacement approval/send no longer 409s off the original order's stale Store history, and each replacement runs as its own workflow instance (own REP order, routing, timeline) — the original order's status never blocks a fresh replacement.
  - **Root cause pinned** (`returnExchange.controller.js`): the "already in Store" rejection messages live here, not in the frontend. Both guards were keyed on `record.orderId` (the **shared original order**) — `createReturnExchange` duplicate guard blocked any `PENDING/FAISAL_APPROVED/IN_PRODUCTION/STORE_RECEIVE/DISPATCH_READY/WAREHOUSE_APPROVED` sibling of the original, and `sendToStore`'s in-flight 409 checked only case status with a live sibling lookup — so a completed/old REP case (e.g. #49502's legacy `FAISAL_APPROVED/STORE` sibling whose REP-49502-2 was already being worked) still rejected the send, and a cancelled/warehouse-rejected sibling could wedge the original forever.
  - **Backend** (`returnExchange.controller.js`): new `REP_ORDER_TERMINAL_STATUSES = ['COMPLETED','DELIVERED','CANCELLED','REJECTED']` + `isReplacementInFlight(caseRecord)` helper — a replacement counts as "in flight" ONLY while its own REP order exists and its `status`/`currentStage` are not terminal; missing/deleted REP orders return `false`. `createReturnExchange` guard now iterates all same-`orderId` REPLACEMENT siblings and blocks only when (a) a sibling is `routedTo === 'FAISAL' && status === 'PENDING' && !replacementOrderId` (still awaiting Faisal review), or (b) a sibling has `replacementOrderId` AND `await isReplacementInFlight(sib)` is true (genuinely active task). `sendToStore` guards now accept BOTH the one-step flow (`routedTo === 'FAISAL'`) and the legacy two-step Approve→Send flow (`routedTo === 'STORE' && status === 'FAISAL_APPROVED' && !replacementOrderId`), keep the `replacementOrderId` early-return, and the in-flight 409 now also requires `await isReplacementInFlight(inFlightCase)` on the sibling (lookup still excludes `CANCELLED/COMPLETED/REPLACEMENT_COMPLETED/WAREHOUSE_REJECTED`).
  - **Frontend** (`FaisalReplacements.jsx` `createAndSend`): mirror guard narrowed to the unambiguous awaiting-Faisal case (`type REPLACEMENT && status PENDING && routedTo FAISAL && !replacementOrderId`); any other state (live REP order, completed, cancelled, deleted) is left to the backend liveness check so the UI never over-blocks.

- **Admin Dashboard outlet statistics accuracy fix** (commit `c38938c` + `4025353` + `7b98ca0`): Admin outlet tabs flow exclusively through `OutletDetailedCard` â†’ `/api/outlet-detailed/:outlet` (AdminDashboard has no direct dashboard wiring). Root causes fixed:
  - **Balance payments bucketed by sale `createdAt`, not `paidAt`** (`outletDetailed.controller.js` + `getSalesDashboard`): the `posBalancePayment` query filtered `posSale: { createdAt: dateWhere }`, violating the payment-date methodology â€” "today" totals missed payments made today on older sales, and out-of-window payments on in-window sales were wrongly included. Both now filter by `paidAt: dateWhere` (with `posSale: { outletName }` for the outlet-scoped one); `getSalesDashboard` also dropped the now-unused `saleIds`-based `in` filter.
  - **`netRevenue` unclamped negative** (`outletDetailed.controller.js` + `getSalesDashboard`): `totalSales - totalReturns` went negative when returns from older sales were processed in-window. Now `Math.max(0, ...)`; per-method `net` in the payment breakdown likewise clamped (`outletDetailed`).
  - **Sales trend omitted balance payments** (`outletDetailed.controller.js` `salesByDay`): now adds `amountPaidNow` to its `paidAt` day (matches `getSalesDashboard`).
  - **`branchPerformance` used raw `grandTotal`** (`pos.controller.js` `getSalesDashboard`): now `saleRevenue(s)` + in-window collected balance payments attributed via the payment's own `posSale.outletName` (robust to sales created before the window).
  - **`balanceOrders` wrong**: previously `paid: ps.grandTotal` (every order-linked sale appeared fully paid) and `totalWithAdvance: grandTotal + advanceAmount` (double-count); worse, the query filtered `orderId: { not: null }` but **all live advance sales link via `orderNumber` (orderId is NULL)** â†’ returned 0. Now matches `OR: [{ orderId: not null }, { orderNumber: not null }]`, computes `paid = advanceAmount + Î£ amountPaidNow` (fully-paid-at-checkout `advance=0 && no balancePayments` â†’ `paid = grandTotal`, `remaining = 0`), `remaining = max(0, grandTotal - paid)`, `totalWithAdvance = grandTotal`; adds `orderNumber` to the payload.
  - **Invoices "Total Sales" card mismatch** (`OutletDetailedCard.jsx:710`): summed only `saleRevenue` (omitting collected balance payments), under-reporting vs the Overview KPI; now uses `summary.totalSales` (Overview KPIs already read `summary.*` = `data.overview`).

### Implemented This Session â€” Routing Sites Wrapped in Atomic Transactions (commit `172fc8a`)
- **Requirement**: Make every order-routing mutation atomic â€” a failure partway through a routing operation (stage completion, destination stage creation, `order.currentStage` update, routingHistory, seen-task reset, audit log, dispatch log) must never leave the order stuck between stages or duplicate a destination task. Previously `outletRouteOrder`, `routeReplacement`, `redispatchOrder`, `dispatchFromProfile`, and `bookCourier` performed their writes as separate non-transactional statements.
- **Tx-aware helpers**: `order-helpers.js` `createAuditLog` now accepts an optional 5th `tx` arg (`const db = tx || prisma`); the local `createAuditLog`/`createDispatchLog` helpers in `dispatch-profile.controller.js` and `dispatch.controller.js` are likewise `(..., tx)`-aware. Callers outside transactions and non-routing callers are unaffected.
- **`outletRouteOrder`** (`outletOrder.controller.js`): target-outlet validation, deadline, recipient-users lookup, orderUpdateData, and audit action/remarks are computed **before** the transaction; the customerTakeDeliver branch (stage complete + order complete + audit + routingHistory) and the main routing path (stage close â†’ destination stage create â†’ order update â†’ routingHistory â†’ seenTask deleteMany â†’ audit) are each wrapped in `prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {...}, { timeout: 30000 })`. `io` emit and `notify.create` stay outside.
- **`routeReplacement`** (`returnExchange.controller.js`): routing section (STORE close, destination stage create, order advance, `tx.user.findMany` recipient lookup, routingHistory, seen reset, both replacement audit logs, returnExchange case sync) wrapped in a 30s transaction. Inventory deduction keeps its own inner `$transaction` and runs before the routing wrapper.
- **`redispatchOrder`** (`returnExchange.controller.js`): active-stage completion loop, DISPATCH stage create, order reset, routingHistory, audit, `tx.user.findMany` + seenTask reset â€” all wrapped in a 30s transaction.
- **`dispatchFromProfile`** (`dispatch-profile.controller.js`): both the FORWARD_TO_FAISAL branch and the full dispatch branch (order/stage updates, OUT_FOR_DELIVERY stage create, routingHistory, seen reset, dispatch log, audit) wrapped in 30s transactions; `io` emit stays outside; recipient users read outside the tx.
- **`bookCourier`** (`dispatch.controller.js`): courier fields update, COURIER_BOOKED audit, OUT_FOR_DELIVERY auto-create, currentStage advance, and ENAMELS routingHistory + seen reset (via `tx.user.findMany`) wrapped in a 30s transaction; `notify.create` stays outside.
- **Verification**: `node --check` clean on all 5 modified controllers + `order-helpers.js`; require-load probe printed `LOAD OK`; `npm run build` in `D:\smart-production\frontend` EXIT=0 (vite 6.4.3, 3153 modules, only the pre-existing POSPrint dynamic/static import warning). Commit `172fc8a` pushed (`4e3386d..172fc8a`); deployed `dpl_8MuCisAYH5xe1UaJwfKqZ9FgvwFB` (`smart-production-v2-641e2qqkg-...`) + re-aliased `smart-production-v2.vercel.app`. **Live verification**: index 200 (`index-CpKv-Rmy.js`), `/api/health` 200, admin login 200 (SUPER_ADMIN), `/api/orders?status=active&limit=3` returns orders, `/api/orders/export?category=all` 200 xlsx (862KB, PK magic).
- **Out of scope (unchanged)**: `customerTakeDeliver` tracking-display short-circuit and live end-to-end timeline verification remain deferred unless requested.

### Implemented This Session â€” Replacement Orders Visible After STORE (stage-aware guard) + Excel Export One Row Per Product (commit `19e60a9`)
- **Requirement**: (1) In-production replacement orders (e.g. `REP-49465`, `currentStage=PRODUCTION`/`status=PENDING` after Store routed it STORE â†’ PRODUCTION) were invisible to Production In/Out and every downstream queue; (2) Admin Orders "Download Excel" emitted blank Product cells for wrapped `productDetails` orders.
- **Root cause 1 â€” blanket REP exclusion**: `getUnseenOrders` (`order.controller.js:3108`), `getDispatchQueue` (`dispatch.controller.js:43`), and `getDispatchProfileOrders` (Khawar branch, `dispatch-profile.controller.js:72`) all carried a blanket `source: { not: 'REPLACEMENT' }` in the Prisma where clause. That was correct only while a REP order sits at the STORE stage (hub-managed in Store Returns/Replacements) â€” once routed onward it is a real order and must surface in Production In/Out, Logo, Dispatch, Delivery, etc. Live DB probe confirmed REP-49465 has a proper `PRODUCTION IN_PROGRESS` stage row yet was excluded from every role view.
- **Fix 1 â€” stage-aware exclusion** (`order.controller.js` `getUnseenOrders`): blanket guard removed; `whereClause.source = { not: 'REPLACEMENT' }` now applied **only when `relevantStages.includes('STORE')`** (i.e. STORE/STORE_EMPLOYEE), with a comment citing REP-49465. `getDispatchQueue` and `getDispatchProfileOrders` simply dropped the blanket guard (dispatch-stage replacements now appear; pipeline comment added). `getStoreDashboardOrders` **keeps** the exclusion deliberately (it is strictly `currentStage: 'STORE'`, where the hub owns REP orders) â€” documented in-code.
- **Fix 2 â€” Production In gate now covers STORE** (`order.controller.js` `requestStageCompletion` ~1029 + `manualRouteOrder` ~2881): the normalization `actualNextStage === 'PRODUCTION' â†’ 'PRODUCTION_ACCEPTANCE'` was limited to Logo stages; added `'STORE'` to the current-stage list so Store "Process & Route" and the replacement hub can no longer bypass Production In straight to Production Out's `PRODUCTION` stage (the route REP-49465 took). This matches the pipeline's own `NEXT_STAGES` (`STANDARD`: STORE â†’ PRODUCTION_ACCEPTANCE).
- **Root cause 2 â€” export blank products**: `getOrdersExport`'s row builder flattened `o.productDetails` with `Array.isArray(items) ? items : []` and read `item.productName` from the top level. Wrapped orders store `[{ productDetails: {â€¦}, quantity, customization, â€¦ }, â€¦]` (ecommerce + REP send-to-store shape), so top-level `item.productName` was undefined â†’ blank Product/Qty/Size/Color cells. Flat outlet shape `[{ name, productType, color, size, quantity, â€¦ }]` and legacy single-object shape also diverged.
- **Fix 2 â€” export row builder** (`order.controller.js` `getOrdersExport` ~812): mirrors the screens' unwrapping (`OrderCard.jsx` `normalizeProduct` / `AllOrders.jsx` `rawPd[0]?.productDetails || rawPd[0]`). Parse string `productDetails`; `Array.isArray(raw) ? raw : (object ? ### Implemented This Session â€” Jail Road POS: Engraving/Logo Charges Separated from Customization at the Data/Calculation Level (commit `705d117`)
- **Requirement**: Fix Jail Road POS so Engraving and Logo Design charges stay completely separate from Customization Charges at the data/calculation level (backend persistence + receipts), not just display level, while the payment total stays identical. Example: 3 Ã— Rs. 300 engraving + Rs. 500 customization must show Engraving: 900, Customization: 500, not Customization: 1,400.
- **Root cause**: `createSale` in `pos.controller.js` (`custCharges = (customization1 ? 500 : 0) + (customization2 ? 1000 : 0) + (nameEngrave ? 300 : 0) + (logoDesign ? 300 : 0)` at 484-488) persisted everything into a single `customizationCharges` field on `PosSaleItem`, so the receipt could only show one combined "Customization" line even though the cart (`POSContext.jsx` 561-565) and `POSCart.jsx` already split the four amounts.

### Implemented This Session â€” Production In Unseen Tasks: All Acceptance Orders Combined, Stable, No Competing Reloads (commit `2f13181`)

Acceptance removes ONLY the accepted order (routes forward to PRODUCTION); new arrivals are additive; removal happens only on a real backend change; no full-list replacement.

New `forceAllAcceptanceIntoUnseen = ['PRODUCTION', 'PRODUCTION_IN'].includes(userRole)` â€” any order at `currentStage === 'PRODUCTION_ACCEPTANCE'` is forced into `unseen` (never hidden by a seenTask) for BOTH the dedicated PRODUCTION_IN profile and the combined PRODUCTION profile. Deleted the old PRODUCTION_IN-only `return res.json({ unseen: all, seen: [] })` branch â€” its purpose is now served by the single generic split (same output, one code path, no stage-role divergence).
- **Frontend hardening** (`MyTasks.jsx`): `tasksSame` now wrapped in `useCallback([])` so `useCache`'s `load` stays referentially stable and the mount effect runs exactly once (no competing reloads over the same React state). All three cache keys bumped `v2:` â†’ `v3:` (`v3:my-tasks:unseen:${role}${empKey}`, `v3:my-tasks:production-returned…`, `v3:my-tasks:active…`) to invalidate stale pre-fix "Outlet-only" snapshots held in old browsers.

### Implemented This Session â€” Resubmit to Store Routes Directly to Store My Tasks + REP Tracking Linkage + REP Number Backfill (commit `8544a27`)
- **Requirement**: Fix the Replacement workflow & tracking issues â€” (1) after Return from Verification â†’ Faisal corrects and picks "Resubmit to Store", the order must land in **Store â†’ My Tasks** immediately (never route back through Verification / Return from Verification); (2) replacement order numbers must be exactly `REP-<originalNumber>` (e.g. `REP-49502`) â€” never independent generated numbers; (3) the replacement must behave as a linked continuation of the original order â€” tracking either number shows both timelines and the real-time location, with one active task at a time.
- **Root cause 1 â€” resubmit misrouted**: `resubmitFromVerification` (`verification.controller.js`) kept `currentStage: 'ORDER_ENTRY'` and created a new VERIFICATION stage, so corrected orders went back through Inventory View Verification again and Faisal's returned list never cleared (tracking derived `RETURNED_FROM_VERIFICATION`).
- **Root cause 2 â€” legacy REP numbers**: two REP- orders had been created with independently generated numbers (`REP-511195`, `REP-116423`) instead of `REP-<original>`.
- **Fix 1 â€” direct Store routing** (`verification.controller.js` `resubmitFromVerification` rewritten, reusing the proven `verifyOrder` Store-routing template): completes PENDING/IN_PROGRESS stages, creates `STORE PENDING` stage, sets `currentStage: 'STORE'` + `status: 'PENDING'`, clears `seenTask` for Store roles (`getRolesForStage('STORE')`), writes routingHistory `ORDER_ENTRYâ†’STORE` with remarks "Resubmitted after correction by â€¦ directly to Store.", creates audit `RESUBMITTED_TO_STORE`, notifies `store_task` â€œOrder Re-submitted to Storeâ€, returns `'Order resubmitted to Store'`.
- **Fix 2 â€” backend tracking linkage** (`order.controller.js`): `ACTION_LABELS` gains `RESUBMITTED_TO_STORE: 'Resubmitted to Store'` (old `RESUBMITTED_AFTER_VERIFICATION` label kept for historical rows); `getOrderTimeline` order select now includes `replacementCaseId`, first-stage label is `'Replacement Order Entered'` when `source === 'REPLACEMENT'`, response includes `source` + `replacementCaseId`; `trackOrder` resolves REPâ†’original (`replacementCaseId` â†’ returnExchange.orderId) and originalâ†’REP (latest returnExchange with non-null `replacementOrderId`), returning `_originalOrder`/`_replacementOrder` each with its own `trackingStatus` (linkage failures caught non-critical).
- **Fix 3 â€” frontend tracking UI** (`OrderTrack.jsx`): `ArrowRightLeft` icon, `linkedOriginal`/`linkedReplacement` state, `loadOrder(query, resetLinked)` fetches the linked order's timeline in place, new "Connected Original/Replacement Order" card with view-timeline buttons, placeholder `JT-836194 or REP-49502`. `OrderEntryContext.jsx` resubmit alert â†’ `'Order updated and sent to Store!'`.
- **Fix 4 â€” live DB backfill** (temp script `prisma/backfill-rep-numbers.js`, since deleted): resolved both legacy rows via `replacementCaseId`/`replacementOrderId` and applied `REP-511195 â†’ REP-49502` (order `4a102937-14f7-42c7-8c8e-df5d1840f2d2`, case `965d76f8-eb51-4446-90d2-dd8a82c03cce`) and `REP-116423 â†’ REP-49465` (order `ec4d9e9a-2281-48c0-8e87-1108e904d0bc`, case `776f2cb8-2560-44cf-abb6-2f7bc17c53b3`); verified via Prisma query, temp script deleted.
- **Verification**: `node --check` + require-load OK on both controllers; `npm run build` EXIT=0 (only pre-existing POSPrint dynamic-import warning; new `OrderTrack-CsANMhCC.js` chunk). Commit `8544a27` pushed (`4c737a3..8544a27`); deployed `dpl_Fww72yDnmsRNfNWF9imWiaK95oHG` (`smart-production-v2-j8v1qq3op-...`) + re-aliased `smart-production-v2.vercel.app`; live index `index-D68GC5Li.js` (no SSO intercept), deployed `OrderTrack-CsANMhCC.js` contains `Replacement Order`/`REP-49502` markers. **Live API verification** (temp script, since deleted): `track REP-49502` â†’ REP order `source REPLACEMENT` / `currentStage LOGO_DESIGN` + `_originalOrder #49502` (COMPLETED); `track #49502` â†’ original + `_replacementOrder REP-49502` (LOGO_DESIGN); `track 49502` â†’ newest match (REP) + original link; timeline for REP-49502 returns `source REPLACEMENT` + `replacementCaseId` + first entry label `Replacement Order Entered` (7 entries); `/api/orders/track/REP-49502` 401 unauthenticated (auth-guarded).

### Implemented This Session - Global System Pause/Resume (backend 423 lock + shared context + control UIs + pause-aware delays) (commit `854fe78`)
- **Requirement**: A single global Pause/Resume control. While paused, EVERY operational mutation across the whole system is blocked server-side (Order Entry, POS sales/payments/invoices/returns, Production, Store, Verification, Logo, Dispatch, Replacement creation/routing, Returns/Exchange/Re-Dispatch, Cancellation, Delivery) - with an authoritative backend guard so direct API calls cannot bypass it. Existing data is never modified/deleted. The lock is DB-persisted so it survives refresh/new tabs/profiles. Delay timers must freeze during pause windows and resume where they stopped; multiple pause/resume cycles supported. Who paused/resumed + timestamps recorded (history + notifications). Admin Dashboard and Software Settings both expose the control; banner shown app-wide.
- **Backend - canonical state store** (`backend/src/utils/systemPause.js`): state lives in `SystemSetting` rows - `SYSTEM_PAUSED` ('true'/'false'), `SYSTEM_PAUSE_INFO` JSON `{pausedBy, pausedById, pausedAt, source}`, `SYSTEM_PAUSE_PERIODS` JSON `[{startedAt, endedAt|null}]` (null = currently paused), `SYSTEM_PAUSE_HISTORY` JSON (newest first, capped 100). `isSystemPaused()` with 5s cache (`invalidateCache()` on write); `pauseSystem`/`resumeSystem` return `{paused, info?, periods}`; `activeElapsedMs(startMs, endMs, periods)` subtracts paused overlap (exported for parity with frontend).
- **Backend - global guard** (`backend/src/middleware/systemPauseGuard.js`): mounted `app.use('/api', systemPauseGuard)` in `app.js` before all route mounts. Every non-GET/HEAD/OPTIONS `/api` request is rejected with **423** `{message: PAUSED_MESSAGE, code: 'SYSTEM_PAUSED'}` while paused. Whitelist prefixes (`/api/auth`, `/api/system`, `/api/feedback`) stay open so Admin can sign back in and resume. Fail-open on state-read errors (a DB hiccup never locks the branch down). Read-only GET traffic is never blocked.
- **Backend - endpoints** (`system.controller.js` + `system.routes.js`): `GET /api/system/state` (any authenticated user - drives the global banner/poll), `POST /api/system/pause` + `POST /api/system/resume` + `GET /api/system/history` (SUPER_ADMIN/ADMIN/SOFTWARE_SETTINGS only). Pause/resume require the acting user's own password (bcrypt verify; 400/401). `broadcastSystemEvent` notifies all 21 roles (`NOTIFY_ROLES`) and emits `system-paused`/`system-resumed` socket events; message variant is source-aware - Software Settings -> `<name> paused the system...` / `<name> resumed...`, Admin Dashboard -> generic `Admin paused the system. All functions are currently stopped.` / `System resumed. All functions are now active.` Source auto-derived from `admin.role` when not passed.
- **Backend - escalation scan skip** (`order.controller.js:544`): `getOrders`' slow overdue-priority escalation scan is now also skipped while paused (was already skipped for `status=delivery`), so the hot path stays fast during a lock.
- **Frontend - shared context** (`frontend/src/context/SystemPauseContext.jsx`): `SystemPauseProvider` polls `GET /api/system/state` every 30s + on window focus, subscribes to socket `system-paused`/`system-resumed`, and exposes `{paused, info, periods, history, loading, refresh, fetchHistory, pause(password), resume(password)}`. `busyRef` guards double-submits. Wired in `App.jsx` inside `NotificationProvider` wrapping `Router`.
- **Frontend - app-wide banner** (`Layout.jsx`): always shows one of two banners - red `SYSTEM PAUSED - All functions are temporarily stopped by Admin.` (with paused-by + timestamp) or green `SYSTEM ACTIVE - All functions have been resumed.` Replaces the old local `/api/admin/pause-status` polling.
- **Frontend - Admin Dashboard** (`AdminDashboard.jsx`): pause modal + header badge now go through the context (`pauseSystem`/`resumeSystem`); header badge text updated to spec; modal subtitle "Stop all functions temporarily. No operations can run while paused."; `getStageDelays(allOrders, null, pausePeriods)`.
- **Frontend - Software Settings** (`SoftwareSettings.jsx`): new **System Pause** tab - status card (paused red / active green, spec texts, paused-by/source/time), Pause/Resume + Confirm buttons with password confirmation form (posts via context), Refresh button, and a Pause/Resume history list (colored dot, action, by, source, formatted time; case-insensitive PAUSE detection - backend stores uppercase `PAUSE`/`RESUME`).
- **Frontend - pause-aware delay timers** (`delayUtils.js` + `AllOrders.jsx`): `activeElapsedMs(startMs, endMs, periods)` subtracts every paused window overlapping the range (reads `p.startedAt || p.pausedAt`, `endedAt` null = still paused). `getDelayInfo` and `getStageDelays` accept an optional `pausePeriods` param (default keeps old behavior); `phaseElapsed`, `totalElapsed`, and `delayDuration` are now computed on **active** (non-paused) time so delays freeze during a pause and resume where they stopped. AllOrders passes `useSystemPause().periods` into both memos.
- **Verification**: `node -e require` OK on all 5 backend files (systemPause, guard, controller, routes, order.controller); `node --check` OK; `npm run build` EXIT=0 (3168 modules, only pre-existing POSPrint dynamic/static import warning); `notify.create(req, {...})` signature verified matching. Commit `854fe78` pushed (`a377865..854fe78`); deployed `dpl_96Uy8Tn8mC6EGH2RUxCh5BpFajSx` (`smart-production-v2-o5zv5iikv-...`) + re-aliased `smart-production-v2.vercel.app`; live index `index-gWwRo9WI.js` (no SSO intercept), index bundle contains `SystemPauseProvider`/`/api/system/state`, lazy chunks confirmed (`AdminDashboard-_p-_SWix.js`, `SoftwareSettings-Dm7h7qV0.js` incl. `Global System Pause`, `delayUtils-D9WihYw7.js` incl. `startedAt`/`Math.min`). **Live smoke test** (temp script, since deleted, admin `admin@enamels.com`): login 200 -> state `{paused:false}` -> mutation while active 404 (not 423) -> **pause 200** (`paused:true`, info+open period) -> state paused -> **mutating call while paused = 423** with exact PAUSED_MESSAGE + `code:'SYSTEM_PAUSED'` -> history shows PAUSE event -> wrong-password resume 401 -> STORE role pause attempt 403 (role gate) + STORE state read 200 -> **resume 200** (`paused:false`, period closed with `endedAt`) -> state unpaused -> mutation works again (404 not 423) -> history shows RESUME event. System left **unpaused** after test. One frontend fix during review: history row action compare was `h.action === 'pause'` vs backend uppercase `PAUSE` -> made case-insensitive.

### Implemented This Session - Profile-Level System Pause (Software Settings picks profiles, guard/banners/timers/notifications scoped, commit `2b0b936`)
- **Requirement**: Upgrade the shipped global pause into a **profile-level pause**. Software Settings chooses which profiles the pause applies to (persisted in backend `SystemSetting`); Admin keeps only **Pause System / Resume System** (no config access) acting per the saved config. Checked profiles stop (tasks, timers, deadlines, mutations blocked server-side); unchecked profiles keep working and their delay timers keep running. Pause -> only affected profiles get notified; resume -> same profiles notified they are active again. Survives refresh/logout/restart/deploy.
- **Canonical profile module** (`backend/src/utils/pauseProfiles.js`, new): 12 profiles - `order_entry` (ORDER_ENTRY), `verification` (INVENTORY_VIEW), `faisal` (FAISAL), `store` (STORE/STORE_EMPLOYEE), `logo_design` (LOGO_DESIGN/LOGO_DESIGN_EMPLOYEE/LOGO_DESIGNER), `production` (PRODUCTION/PRODUCTION_IN/PRODUCTION_OUT), `dispatch` (DISPATCH/MAIN_EMPLOYEE), `delivery` (DELIVERY_BOY), `outlet_johar`/`outlet_abbottabad`/`outlet_jail`/`outlet_other` (OUTLET resolved by `user.name` substring johar/abbottabad/jail -> else `outlet_other`, mirroring `getOutletName`). Exports `PROFILES, CONTROL_ROLES (SUPER_ADMIN/ADMIN/CEO/SOFTWARE_SETTINGS), DEFAULT_PROFILE_KEYS, VALID_KEYS, resolveProfileKey(user) (returns 'control' for admin roles, outlet-key, role-map key, or null), rolesForProfiles(keys)`.
- **Backend state store** (`systemPause.js`): new `SYSTEM_PAUSE_PROFILES` SystemSetting (JSON array; missing/empty = **all profiles** = backward-compatible global pause). `getPauseProfiles`/`setPauseProfiles` (VALID_KEYS-filtered). Every pause period now snapshots `{startedAt, endedAt, profiles}`; `activeElapsedMs(startMs, endMs, periods, profileKey)` skips windows that don't include the caller's profile (legacy windows without `profiles` still count for everyone). New `isProfilePaused(profileKey)` = paused && selected && profile not 'control'.
- **Backend guard** (`systemPauseGuard.js`): still mounted before `authenticate` in `app.js`, but now self-decodes the Bearer JWT (`jsonwebtoken` -> `{role, name}`), resolves the caller's profile, and returns 423 **only when** `isSystemPaused()` && `isProfilePaused(profileKey)`. Unauthenticated/invalid-token/unknown-profile fail open (never 423 - `authenticate` handles auth later). Whitelist `/api/auth`, `/api/system`, `/api/feedback` unchanged.
- **Backend endpoints** (`system.controller.js` + `system.routes.js`): `GET /api/system/state` now also returns `affected` (caller profile paused+selected), `myProfile`, `profileDefs` (key+label). New `GET /api/system/pause-profiles` + `PUT /api/system/pause-profiles` (SUPER_ADMIN/ADMIN/SOFTWARE_SETTINGS) - empty array resets to all-profiles global pause. `pauseSystem`/`resumeSystem` snapshot `state.profiles` into the period; notifications via `rolesForProfiles(selected)` filtered of CONTROL_ROLES with spec messages - pause: `System is stopped by Admin for your profile. All functions are temporarily stopped.`, resume: `System has been resumed by Admin. All functions are now active.` (excluded profiles get nothing). Resume notifies the profiles from the period just closed. Socket `system-paused`/`system-resumed` broadcast kept.
- **Frontend context** (`SystemPauseContext.jsx`): state now includes `affected`, `profiles`, `profileDefs`, `myProfile` from `/api/system/state`; new `saveProfiles(keys)` (PUT); all exposed through `useSystemPause`.
- **Frontend - Software Settings** (`SoftwareSettings.jsx`): new **System Pause Settings** panel in the `system` tab - checkbox grid of all 12 profiles (pre-selected from saved config), Save (PUT), **All Profiles (Global Pause)** reset button, live selected-count. Status card text updated to "selected profiles" wording. Admin keeps only the Pause/Resume button (no config UI).
- **Frontend - banners** (`Layout.jsx`): red banner only when `affected` (text "temporarily stopped for your profile"); while system paused but caller unaffected a muted indigo banner "System paused for other profiles - your profile continues running normally"; green ACTIVE banner otherwise. Admin Dashboard badge text updated to "selected profiles".
- **Frontend - timers** (`delayUtils.js` + `AllOrders.jsx` + `AdminDashboard.jsx`): `activeElapsedMs`, `getDelayInfo`, `getStageDelays` accept an optional `profileKey`; AllOrders/AdminDashboard pass `myProfile` (control = null = global view), so only pause windows covering the caller's profile freeze its delay timers.
- **Verification**: `node --check` + require-load OK on all 5 backend files; pure-logic test 11/11 (resolveProfileKey cases + rolesForProfiles + activeElapsedMs global/store/production/other scoping); `npm run build` EXIT=0 (built in 44.80s). Commit `2b0b936` pushed (`854fe78..2b0b936`); deployed `dpl_A2EzxvRPkafQyiJ3abXHXjuWqwNP` (`smart-production-v2-bvwek01gq-...`) + re-aliased `smart-production-v2.vercel.app`. **Live smoke test** (temp script `smoke-pause-profiles.js`, since deleted, `admin@enamels.com`/`store@enamels.com`/`production@enamels.com`, password `enamels1212`): index no SSO intercept -> login 3/3 -> save profiles `['store']` -> state admin `myProfile=control affected=false profiles=['store'] defs=12` -> **pause 200** -> **store mutation = 423** (`code:'SYSTEM_PAUSED'`) while **production mutation = 400 (NOT 423)** -> state store `affected=true` / production `affected=false` -> **resume 200** (period closed with `profiles:['store']`) -> store mutation = 400 (NOT 423) -> history PAUSE event carries `profiles:['store']` -> config reset to all-profiles (12) -> **16/16 PASS, system left unpaused**. ### Implemented This Session - Centralized Pause/Resume Control in the Global Top Nav Bar (commit `9d58298`)
- **Requirement**: Move the Pause/Resume System control out of the AdminDashboard header and the Software Settings system tab into ONE central control in the global top navigation bar (`Layout.jsx` header). Authorized users (SUPER_ADMIN/ADMIN/SOFTWARE_SETTINGS) see the live status immediately: active -> green **Pause System** button, paused -> red **Resume System** button. No backend behavior changes; profile-level pause configuration stays in Software Settings; Admin gets no config access.
- **Frontend** (`frontend/src/components/SystemPauseControl.jsx`, new): self-contained top-bar control mounted once in `Layout.jsx` header actions (before the FAISAL role block). Uses `useAuth` + `useSystemPause` (`paused`, `info`, `pause(password)`, `resume(password)`) + `react-hot-toast` + `framer-motion`. Gated on `CONTROL_ROLES = ['SUPER_ADMIN', 'ADMIN', 'SOFTWARE_SETTINGS']` (same as backend). Shows a password-confirmation modal before pausing/resuming (backend re-verifies the acting user's password); while paused it also shows `pausedBy`/source/timestamp; success/error toasts read `res.message`/`err.message` from the backend.

"Send Alert" notify button retained.
- **`SoftwareSettings.jsx` cleaned**: removed `pausePassword`/`pauseBusy` state and `pauseSystem`/`resumeSystem` destructure; the old "Global System Pause / Resume" card with inline password form replaced by a status-only "System Pause Status" card that points to the top-bar button and keeps Refresh + the red/green status banner; System Pause Settings helper text now says profiles are used from the top bar. Config grid + Pause/Resume History remain.
- **Verification**: `npm run build` EXIT=0 (built in 39.25s; new `AdminDashboard-DajvTk2s.js` local chunk; pause labels now in the main index chunk from the new component). Commit `9d58298` pushed (`8aa95fd..9d58298`); deployed `dpl_12K4PZRXxkXenETHvnYXmxunrhjD` (`smart-production-v2-m3tsnz7ja-...`) + re-aliased `smart-production-v2.vercel.app` (4s). ### Fixed This Session â€” Admin Dashboard `ReferenceError: selectedInvoice is not defined` (commit `c48263d`)
- **Symptom**: Admin Dashboard (SUPER_ADMIN) crashed with `ReferenceError: selectedInvoice is not defined` thrown from the deployed `AdminDashboard` chunk (user reported stack `at wr (AdminDashboard-…js)`), and ErrorBoundary showed "Something went wrong". Trigger: opening an outlet tab's **Bank Deposits** section.
- **Root cause**: `OutletDetailedCard.jsx` has a **separate sibling component** `BankDepositsSection` (line ~1535) rendered by the parent's `{activeSection === 'bank-deposits' && <BankDepositsSection …/>}`. The clickable-invoice modal from `9a36dc6` — `<InvoiceDetailModal sale={selectedInvoice} onClose={() => setSelectedInvoice(null)} />` — had been placed at the END of `BankDepositsSection` (line 1641) instead of in `OutletDetailedCard` where `const [selectedInvoice, setSelectedInvoice] = useState(null)` is declared (line 298). `BankDepositsSection` is a sibling, not a nested function, so those identifiers were out of scope → every render of the Bank Deposits section threw the ReferenceError. esbuild keeps such out-of-scope names un-minified (that's why `selectedInvoice` appeared verbatim as a free reference in the production chunk).
- **Fix** (`OutletDetailedCard.jsx`): moved the `<InvoiceDetailModal …/>` invocation out of `BankDepositsSection` into the parent `OutletDetailedCard` render (right after the bank-deposits conditional, before the closing `</div>`), where the state lives. The modal is a fixed overlay so its position in the tree is irrelevant to display.
- **Verification**: local `npm run build` EXIT=0 — new `AdminDashboard-*.js` has **0** free `selectedInvoice`/`setSelectedInvoice` references (properly minified locals); committed `c48263d` pushed (`0c7c987..c48263d`); deployed `dpl_AMJAQMnNWQfQWmFKbZhcY5VudZWT` (`smart-production-v2-m0zu1feec-…`) + re-aliased `smart-production-v2.vercel.app`; live index `index-Cpc99GVA.js` (no SSO intercept), live chunk `AdminDashboard-Dr8_AWXL.js` has 0 free `selectedInvoice` refs and still renders `sale:` (InvoiceDetailModal intact).

### Implemented This Session â€” Check in Inventory Accuracy Fix + Admin Cancellation Pending/Cancelled Tabs (commit `14a1fdd`)
- **Request**: (1) Store "Check in Inventory" was reporting false 0 "Available" for products that clearly had stock; (2) Admin Order Cancellation default must show only active/pending cancellation requests, with historical cancelled orders in a separate CANCELLED tab (Order #, Customer, Cancellation Reason, Requested By, Approved By, Cancellation Date, Cancellation Time, Final Status); (3) cancelled orders must be removed from ALL pending-task queues.
- **Pending-task audit (complete)**: every active queue already excludes `CANCELLED`/final statuses — `getUnseenOrders` (order.controller.js:2759, `notIn ['COMPLETED','DELIVERED','CANCELLED','REJECTED']`, OUTLET restricted to `ORDER_ENTRY`), `getStoreDashboardOrders` (:3608), `getDispatchQueue` (dispatch.controller.js:43, `currentStage: { notIn: [...] }`), `getDispatchProfileOrders` (dispatch-profile.controller.js:42), `getComeFromProduction` (outletOrder.controller.js:1154), `getOutletTasks` (:616), `getInDispatchOrders` (:1125). `getDeliveryOrders` (delivery.controller.js:25) intentionally includes `CANCELLED` in its status OR — that's the delivery-boy history source, not a pending-task queue (a cancelled order's `currentStage` is `CANCELLED`, so it never lands in the Pending tab). Verification queues were the one leak and shipped earlier in `8f398cb`.
- **Fix 1 â€” Check in Inventory root cause** (`checkOrderInventory`, order.controller.js:2368, route `GET /api/orders/:orderId/inventory-check`): old code used `allInvItems.find(inv => inv.name.toLowerCase().includes(productType))` → picked the FIRST duplicate-name item (e.g. Order 50363 "Sprinter Men" Navy Blue/L → 0 because a differently-colored "Sprinter Men Grey Nova" matched first, while the exact "Sprinter Men" row has 4), summed only variants with `stock > 0`, and never normalized whitespace ("BunFit Cap" order colors carry a leading space, e.g. `" Cheetah"` → false 0). Rewrote: batch-fetch keys on the order's real product `name` (`name || productType`); candidates = all contains-matches; **exact-name matches preferred** when any exist; stock aggregated across ALL matching items' variants (and flat `stock` fallback for variant-less rows) with color/size matched case-insensitively + trimmed. Classification `inventory` when `availableQty >= requiredQty`, else `production` (genuinely-out-of-stock items still report 0 honestly). Live run of the deployed-equivalent handler on real STORE orders: Clogs Flufftail White/38-39=1, Crown Men Zinc/M=5, Sprinter Cap Unisex "Propofol"=0 (real OOS), Crown Women Navy/XXL=0 — no more false 0s.
- **Fix 2 â€” Admin cancellation tabs** (`frontend/src/pages/OrderCancellations.jsx`): restructured into **Pending** (default, amber) and **Cancelled** (emerald) tabs fed by three parallel fetches (`?status=PENDING` / `APPROVED` / `REJECTED`); default view shows ONLY active requests; Cancelled tab is a history table with Order #, Customer (+phone), Cancellation Reason, Requested By, Approved By, Cancellation Date, Cancellation Time, Final Status (red CANCELLED badge from `order.status`). Stats row (Pending/Cancelled/Rejected), search, refresh, approve/reject modal unchanged. No backend changes needed — `getCancellationRequests` (order.controller.js:1402) already returns `reason`/`requestedByName`/`decidedByName`/`decidedAt`/`decisionNote`/`orderNumber` + nested `order.status`.
- **Verification**: `node -e require order.controller.js` OK; logic simulation 3/3 (Sprinter Men 4, BunFit Cap leading-space 6, not-found); frontend build EXIT=0 (new `OrderCancellations-BxGvdEBv.js` local chunk); commit `14a1fdd` pushed (`8f398cb..14a1fdd`); deployed `dpl_FLtcyoRFKewqed2iLMSK34t3UWiq` (`smart-production-v2-iprn4amoy-...`) + re-aliased `smart-production-v2.vercel.app`; live index serves app (no SSO intercept, `index-CMwb0H8z.js`), deployed chunk `OrderCancellations-BcG4nEk_.js` contains `Pending (` / `Cancelled (` / `Approved By` / `Cancellation Time` / `Final Status`; `GET /api/orders/cancellation-requests` 401 unauthenticated (function live + auth-guarded); real `checkOrderInventory` handler run against live Supabase produced correct quantities on 3 active STORE orders.

### Implemented This Session â€” Deleted Order #49996 Restored (from DeletedOrder snapshot, placed at end of list)
- **Request**: User accidentally deleted order **#49996** ("I ACCIDENTLY DELETE THIS ORDER SO RESTORE IT AND PLACE IT WHERE IS IS AAT LAST PLACE") â€” user confirmed via question the restored order should appear at the **very end/bottom** of the orders list, not its original chronological spot.
- **Root cause / no-restore-path**: `deleteOrder` (`order.controller.js:1436`, `DELETE /api/orders/:orderId`, SUPER_ADMIN/ADMIN) restores inventory, writes a `DeletedOrder` snapshot, then **hard-deletes** the child rows (`orderEditRequest`, `orderStage`, `auditLog`) and the `Order` row itself. No restore endpoint exists â€” recovery had to be done as a direct DB insert mirroring `createOrder`'s structure.
- **Snapshot recovered** (`scripts/find_deleted_49996.js`, since deleted): `DeletedOrder` id `f1904d39-b168-404f-98ec-c9b24bf9efee`, `orderNumber "#49996"`, `source "ONLINE ORDER"`, customer **shahid saeed**, phone `+92 300 9876810`, `totalPrice 7700`, deleted 2026-08-07 by Admin; `productDetails` = 1 item Velora Men/Sprinter/Black/L half-sleeve qty 1 unit 7500 (7700 = 7500 + 200 delivery), Urdu special note + sizeData preserved in the snapshot JSON.
- **Restore** (`scripts/restore_49996.js`, since deleted): Prisma `order.create` with `orderNumber "#49996"`, customer name/phone, `productDetails` from snapshot, `totalPrice 7700`, `deliveryCharges 200`, `source "INTERNAL"`, `outletName "ONLINE ORDER"`, `createdById = Faisal c5d04588-...` (creator convention for ONLINE-ORDER orders so it shows in the online list), `currentStage "ORDER_ENTRY"`, `status "PENDING"`, **`createdAt = now`** (places it at the very bottom â€” `sortByPriority` is priority asc then `createdAt` asc, so newest = last). Also created a COMPLETED ORDER_ENTRY `OrderStage` + `ORDER_CREATED` audit log ("Order restored after accidental deletion..."). **No inventory restore needed** â€” `deleteOrder` already restored inventory at deletion time; keeping it at ORDER_ENTRY (not auto-routed to STORE) prevents any re-deduction.
- **Verification**: `node scripts/restore_49996.js` â†’ order `6edc48a1-...` created with stage ORDER_ENTRY/COMPLETED + audit ORDER_CREATED; DB check mirroring backend `sortByPriority` over all 1392 INTERNAL orders â†’ `#49996` is index 1391 = **last position** (`is at last position: true`); live API `GET /api/orders?search=49996` â†’ `1 hit: #49996 | shahid saeed | stage=ORDER_ENTRY | status=PENDING | total=7700`. Temp scripts deleted after use. DB-only change â€” no code changed, no deploy required.

### Implemented This Session â€” In Dispatch Clear Balance Workflow (collect remaining balance on Dispatch Slip)
- **Requirement**: In **In Dispatch** (`/in-dispatch`), clicking **Print Dispatch Slip** on an order that has a balance must prompt to **enter the balance amount being collected**; the balance is then cleared, the order shows **PAID** in In Dispatch, and the outstanding balance disappears from the **POS Dashboard balance section**.
- **Backend**:
  - `pos.controller.js`: exported `generateBalanceReceiptNumber` (was module-private) so the In Dispatch flow reuses the exact `BP-YYYYMMDD-XXXX` receipt format.
  - `inDispatch.controller.js` `getInDispatchOrders`: `_payment` now also carries `posSaleId` (the linked POS sale id) so the frontend/backend can target the exact sale for the payment.
  - `inDispatch.controller.js` `clearBalance` (new, `POST /api/in-dispatch/orders/:id/clear-balance`): JOHAR TOWN guard; resolves the linked POS sale by `orderId` FK then `orderNumber` (same as `getInDispatchOrders`); rejects unlinked sales / Faisal Take / `amountPaidNow <= 0` / already-fully-paid / over-remaining (mirrors `pos.controller.js` `payBalance` guard rails); validates `CASH_ONLINE` cash+online split; creates a `PosBalancePayment` with `receiptNumber`, original invoice ref, `previouslyPaidAmount`/`remainingBalanceBeforePayment`/`outstandingBalanceAfterPayment`, method, cash/online splits, `cashierName`, `paidAt` â€” exactly the POS balance-payment ledger, so the **POS Dashboard balance section clears automatically**; writes a `BALANCE_CLEARED` audit log on the order; invalidates the `pos:` cache family + emits `inventory-updated` (balancePayment) socket event.
  - `inDispatch.routes.js`: `POST /orders/:id/clear-balance` (authenticate) alongside the existing `/orders/:id/route`.
- **Frontend** (`InDispatch.jsx`):
  - Queue-card actions: new amber **"Clear Balance â€” â‚¨remaining"** button when `order._payment.linked && remaining > 0.01`; **Print Dispatch Slip** now goes through `handlePrintSlipClick` â€” if a linked balance remains it opens the Clear Balance modal, otherwise prints directly (unchanged).
  - **Clear Balance modal**: order/customer/total/paid/remaining summary; balance amount input (pre-filled with full remaining, partial allowed); payment method picker (CASH / ONLINE / CARD / CASH_ONLINE with cash+online split inputs when CASH_ONLINE); **"Clear Balance & Print Slip"** posts to the endpoint, on success updates the in-memory `_payment` (paid += amount, remaining âˆ’= amount, status â†’ Paid), toasts, refetches the queue, then prints the Dispatch Slip with the updated PAID payment; **"Skip â€” Print Slip Only"** prints without clearing.
- **Verification**: `node -e require` OK on all 3 backend files; `npm run build` 0 errors (new `InDispatch-BCZP9GpG.js` chunk containing `clear-balance` / `Clear Balance` / `Print Slip Only`); commit `e87bbd2` pushed; deployed `dpl_5MghYThJjW7GYVzXDwS6DvhQFseE` (`smart-production-v2-gagplpt1g-...`) + re-aliased `smart-production-v2.vercel.app`; live index `index-DsvOeGaI.js` â†’ `InDispatch-D_pBSnvU.js` contains all clear-balance markers.

'Johar Town' : order.outletName`).

### Implemented This Session â€” Complete System Review & Critical Fixes (atomic POS receipts, transactional refunds/transfers, audit-approval skip tracking, structured error logging)
- **Requirement**: Full-system review pass â€” (1) verify inventory audit approval applies immediately/all-products/+andâˆ’/branch-scoped/never-twice/complete-log with failures logged (not silently skipped); (2) fix Store (warehouse) POS "sales sometimes not created"; (3) verify workflow transitions update status+timestamps+dashboard+logs+notify; (4) verify inventory consistency across purchases/production/transfers/sales/returns/audits; (5) broad audit; (6) improve error logging (module/user/branch/order/product/time/error/stack, no silent failures).
- **ROOT CAUSE found for "sales sometimes not created"** â€” `generateReceiptNumber` in both `warehouse.controller.js` (`WRH-â€¦`) and `pos.controller.js` (`RCP-â€¦`) was a **non-atomic read-then-increment** against the `PosSale.receiptNumber` **@unique** column. Two concurrent cashiers/browsers both read the same `findFirst` max and generated the same number â†’ the loser's `posSale.create` threw a unique-constraint error â†’ 500 "Failed to create sale". The frontend already retries once with a `clientRequestId` idempotency key (and always toasts errors), so users saw exactly "sometimes fails on first attempt, works on retry".
- **Fix â€” atomic per-day sequence** (`PosSaleSequence` model: `@@unique([prefix])` + `upsert` `{ create: { nextValue: seed }, update: { nextValue: { increment: 1 } } }`, matching the existing `InvoiceSequence`/`AlterationSequence` pattern). `generateReceiptNumber` (both controllers) now: seed = max existing receipt number for today's prefix + 1 (so numbering continues seamlessly â€” live today: WRH-â€¦-00072 / RCP-â€¦-00037 â†’ next sale gets 73/38), then atomic upsert. Two concurrent calls can never return the same number (unique prefix serializes; the loser increments). Pushed to DB via `prisma db push`.
- **Transactional refunds** â€” `refundInvoice` (warehouse + outlet) ran stock-restore + `posReturn.create` + `refundedAt` stamp as separate statements OUTSIDE a transaction: a mid-loop failure left partial stock restored with the sale NOT marked refunded, so a retry would double-restore. Now wrapped in a 30s `$transaction` with an in-tx `refundedAt` re-check guard (race-safe double-refund protection).
- **Transactional transfer acceptance** â€” `acceptTransfer` (`transfer.controller.js`) processed every item (source decrement + destination increment + create) outside a transaction, then flipped status to COMPLETED: a mid-loop failure would let a retry double-apply earlier items. Now the whole loop + COMPLETED status update is one 30s `$transaction`; validation failures are thrown as typed errors (`.validation = true`) and return 400 (rolled back), everything else returns 500 with error logging.
- **Audit-approval hardening** (`approveAudit`): added live-target verification BEFORE building the raw-SQL CASE updates â€” OUTLET `OutletInventory` ids and `InventoryItem` ids are checked to still exist; missing targets are now **excluded from `InventoryAdjustmentLog`** (previously a missing row would produce a fake "applied" log while the CASE silently no-op'd) and surfaced via `errorLogger.logWarn` + a new `skipped` array in the response. All 7 review points verified: immediate apply, all changed products processed, +/âˆ’ via absolute `newQty = max(0, physicalQty)`, branch-scoped (OUTLET rows vs WAREHOUSE items), idempotent (`SUBMITTED`-only guard), every applied adjustment logged, failures logged (not silently skipped).
- **Structured error logging** â€” new `SystemLog` model (`module`, `level`, `userId`, `userName`, `outletName`, `context`, `message`, `stack`, `createdAt`, indexed on `[module, createdAt]` + `[level]`) + `backend/src/utils/errorLogger.js` (`logError` â†’ console.error + persisted; `logWarn` â†’ persisted; never throws). Wired into: warehouse `createSale`/`createReturn`/`refundInvoice`, outlet `createSale`/`createReturn`/`refundInvoice`, audit `approve`/`reject`, transfer `accept` â€” every catch now records module/user/branch/context(order#, saleId, productId)/error/stack.
- **Workflow transitions verified** (`requestStageCompletion`): current stage â†’ COMPLETED + `completedAt`, next stage created PENDING + `deadlineAt`, `order.currentStage`/`status` updated, `routingHistory` + `AuditLog` written, `notify.create` to the next department role, `order-updated` socket emitted. OUT_FOR_DELIVERY completion â†’ COMPLETED + revenue recorded. (Prior sessions already fixed ordering of validation-before-complete and defensive `stages: { some }` filters.)
- **Verification**: `prisma db push` synced (`PosSaleSequence`/`SystemLog` tables live); `node -e require` OK on all 5 changed backend files; `npm run build` 0 errors; live deploy + re-alias to `smart-production-v2.vercel.app`; live smoke â€” admin login 200, `pos-lock` JT `{locked:false}`, JT inventory 1599 rows; DB check â€” new tables present (0 rows), today's max receipts WRH-â€¦-00072/RCP-â€¦-00037, atomic upsert proven via in-transaction rollback test (no residue). Commit `68bcfb8` pushed + deployed (`smart-production-v2-npv5k26vm-â€¦`) + aliased.

### Implemented This Session â€” Order Tracking Shows Real Workflow Status (Verification / Returned / Resubmit)
- **Root cause**: `createOrder` hardcodes `currentStage: 'ORDER_ENTRY'` and skips auto-advance to STORE when `goForVerification` is true â€” so orders sitting in the Verification queue (or returned to Faisal) were still displayed as **Order Entry** on the tracking page. The pipeline had no VERIFICATION stage, and `resubmitFromVerification` bypassed verification entirely (straight to STORE).
- **Backend** (`order.controller.js`):
  - New `getTrackingStatus(order)` helper â€” derives the real workflow location for display: `goForVerification && !verifiedAt && !verificationReturnedAt` â†’ `'VERIFICATION'`; `verificationReturnedAt` set â†’ `'RETURNED_FROM_VERIFICATION'`; otherwise `currentStage`.
  - `trackOrder` response now includes `trackingStatus` (computed at L3481); `getOrderTimeline` also fetches the order and returns `trackingStatus`.
  - `STAGE_LABELS_MAP` gained `VERIFICATION: 'Verification'`; `ACTION_LABELS` gained `RETURNED_FOR_CORRECTION: 'Returned from Verification'` and `RESUBMITTED_AFTER_VERIFICATION: 'Resubmitted after Verification'` (previously fell back to raw title-case).
- **Backend** (`verification.controller.js` `resubmitFromVerification`): Now routes back to **Verification** instead of Store â€” keeps `currentStage: 'ORDER_ENTRY'` (tracking derives VERIFICATION), removes the STORE stage creation + STORE seen-task clearing, records routing history `ORDER_ENTRY â†’ VERIFICATION`, writes `RESUBMITTED_AFTER_VERIFICATION` audit, notifies `INVENTORY_VIEW` at `/verification`. The order reappears in the pending-verification queue; after the second verification pass, `verifyOrder` moves it to STORE as usual.
- **Frontend** (`OrderTrack.jsx`):
  - `STAGE_LABELS`/`STAGE_ORDER`/`STAGE_ICONS` gained `VERIFICATION` (ShieldCheck, pipeline position after ORDER_ENTRY) and `RETURNED_FROM_VERIFICATION` label.
  - Current Stage card + pipeline now use `order.trackingStatus` (falls back to `currentStage`); VERIFICATION shows as active pulsing chip; RETURNED_FROM_VERIFICATION shows ORDER_ENTRY active + red "Verification (Returned)" chip.
  - Red "Returned from Verification" banner with return time + reason; PENDING/VERIFIED/RETURNED badge now red for returned orders.
  - Timeline already rendered verification audit entries chronologically (SENT_FOR_VERIFICATION â†’ VERIFICATION_PENDING â†’ ORDER_VERIFIED â†’ RETURNED_FOR_CORRECTION / RESUBMITTED_AFTER_VERIFICATION) with date/time/actor.
- **Frontend** (`OrderEntryContext.jsx`): Resubmit success message now reads "Order updated and sent back for verification!".
- **Verification**: `node -e require` OK on both controllers; `npm run build` 0 errors (new `OrderTrack-*.js` chunk).

### Fixed This Session â€” Dispatch Accept â†’ Seen Task (orders stuck in Unseen queue)
- **Symptom**: Dispatch profile orders JT-350879 and JT-138140 appeared in the Dispatch queue; clicking Accept showed "Accepted", but the order never moved to **Seen Tasks** â€” it disappeared from Unseen and could not be marked as dispatched.
- **Root cause (confirmed with live production data)**: Both orders had gone through DISPATCH more than once, leaving **two DISPATCH stage records** â€” an old `COMPLETED` one (startedAt=null) and a fresh `PENDING`/`IN_PROGRESS` one. `getDispatchProfileOrders` and `getDispatchQueue` both classified acceptance via `order.stages.find(s => s.stageName === 'DISPATCH')`, which returns the **OLDEST** record â€” the COMPLETED stage with `startedAt=null` â€” so `isAccepted` was always false and accepted orders stayed in Unseen. `acceptDispatchOrder` had the same flaw: it targeted `order.stages?.[0]` (the old COMPLETED stage), so the `status === 'PENDING'` branch never fired and the fresh stage was never flipped to `IN_PROGRESS`; only `dispatchOfficer` was set. Live check: `dispatchOfficer='Faisal'` + a genuine `IN_PROGRESS` DISPATCH stage, yet the order showed **UNSEEN** (Faisal unseen=78/seen=71).
- **Fix 1 â€” classification** (`dispatch-profile.controller.js` Khawar L94 + Faisal L138): replaced `stages.find(...)` with `(order.stages || []).filter(s => s.stageName === 'DISPATCH')` and take the **last** entry (stages are `createdAt: 'asc'`), and treat a set `order.dispatchOfficer` as an accepted signal: `isAccepted = (latestStage?.startedAt != null) || order.dispatchOfficer != null`.
- **Fix 2 â€” accept handler** (`acceptDispatchOrder`): `include.stages` now ordered `createdAt: 'asc'`; targets the **latest** DISPATCH stage. If the latest stage is missing or terminal (`COMPLETED`/`REJECTED`/`CANCELLED`) â†’ creates a fresh `IN_PROGRESS` stage with `startedAt` + deadline; if `PENDING` â†’ flips to `IN_PROGRESS` + `startedAt`; else (already IN_PROGRESS/WAITING_APPROVAL) â†’ ensures `startedAt` is present. Idempotent for re-accepts.
- **Fix 3 â€” dashboard** (`getDispatchDashboard` `trackingData`): `assignedAt` now uses the latest DISPATCH stage.
- **Fix 4 â€” generic Dispatch Center** (`dispatch.controller.js` `getDispatchQueue`): same latest-stage + `dispatchOfficer` accepted-signal fix so the Dispatch Center page is consistent with the profile.
- **Verification**: `node -e require` OK on both controllers. Commit `d41a7d7` deployed (`dpl_7KBcREthdfH64Utues41rg13H4fL`) + re-aliased `smart-production-v2.vercel.app`. Live check post-fix: Faisal counts unseen=78â†’59, seen=71â†’90 (19 previously-misclassified orders corrected); JT-350879 and JT-138140 both now in **SEEN** for Faisal.

- **Requirement**: Admin Dashboard gets a Delay Orders breakdown card with per-stage filters; clicking a stage shows only delayed orders stuck in that stage (Order Entry, Verification, Store, Production, Logo Design, Dispatch, Store Receive, Out for Delivery + any stage where an order can stay pending). Per delayed order show order number, customer name, current workflow stage, assigned user, stage entry date/time, total delay duration, current status.
- **Shared utility** (`frontend/src/utils/delayUtils.js`, new): single source of truth moved out of AllOrders â€” `STAGE_DEPARTMENTS`, `DELAY_REASONS` (incl. `Verification`), `STAGE_LABELS`, `STAGE_ORDER` (canonical chip order), `FALLBACK_STAGE_HOURS` (incl. `VERIFICATION: 24`), `stageLabel`, `fmtDuration` (<24h â†’ Hours, â‰¥24h â†’ Days), `getEffectiveStage` (derives `'VERIFICATION'` from `goForVerification && !verifiedAt && !verificationReturnedAt`, same as backend `getTrackingStatus`), `getDelayInfo` (deadline-based; for verification falls back to ORDER_ENTRY stage `completedAt` as phase start), `getStageDelays` (per-stage breakdown sorted by STAGE_ORDER then count desc).

- **AdminDashboard.jsx**: `delayBreakdown = useMemo(() => getStageDelays(allOrders))`; `stats.delayedOrders` is now real (`delayBreakdown.reduce(...)`); landing page gets a dedicated **Delay Orders** card (red border, Clock icon, "View All Delayed (N)" â†’ `/orders` with `filterCategory: 'delayed'`) containing a per-stage chip grid (label + count, each chip â†’ `/orders` with `{ filterCategory: 'delayed', filterDelayStage: <stage> }`); empty state "No delayed orders right now"; old "Delayed Stages" stat card renamed "Delayed Orders" and now navigates with `{ filterCategory: 'delayed' }` instead of `{ filterUrgent: true }`.
- **Verification**: `npm run build` 0 errors (new `delayUtils-BJOObKu2.js` chunk; only pre-existing POSPrint dynamic-import warning). Commit `ed16524`. Deployed `dpl_9E5DCi6dJsgr6aH14GT4vC6DNxue` + re-aliased `smart-production-v2.vercel.app` (index `index-g8NlKTIt.js` serves `AllOrders-BYu6EAqW` / `delayUtils-BJOObKu2` / `AdminDashboard-BeFcbIvb`). Live API check against production orders: delay breakdown computed successfully â€” DISPATCH 76, OUT_FOR_DELIVERY 220, PRODUCTION 6, VERIFICATION 6, PRODUCTION_ACCEPTANCE 4, LOGO_DESIGN 4, STORE 2, ORDER_ENTRY 2.

### Fixed This Session â€” "Branch inventory not updated after approval" (actually applied) + Duplicate Adjustment Logs on Audit Approve (162Ã—2)
- **Investigation of the reported bug**: User's Johar Town audit (AUD-0007, 1598 variants, 385 scanned) was approved but the user reported branch inventory was never updated. Root-caused with read-only diagnostics against the live Supabase DB + live API:
  - **The inventory WAS updated.** 154/162 changed `OutletInventory` rows now have `stock` EXACTLY equal to the approved `physicalQty`; the other **8 differ by exactly 1â€“2 units**, and each was proven against `PosSaleItem` to be a legitimate POST-approval POS sale (e.g. Crown Men Olive C: audit physical 500 â†’ live 499 after 1 sold; Crown Men Grey L: 5 â†’ 3 after 2 sold; 26 Johar Town sales occurred after `approvedAt`). `approvedAt = 2026-08-03T20:40 UTC` â€” this was our fixed-build re-approval; the user's own earlier approval attempt had 500'd on the old deployment, which is why they saw no change at the time.
  - Live `GET /api/pos/inventory?outlet=Johar Town` (admin token) confirms the production API now serves the corrected stock.
- **Root cause of the REAL bug found**: `approveAudit` (rewritten in `a8dd22c`) ran BOTH the new interactive `$transaction` (SQL CASE updates + `inventoryAdjustmentLog.createMany` + statusâ†’APPROVED) AND the leftover legacy batch `prisma.$transaction([...])` block that repeated `createMany` + status update. Result: **every approved audit created adjustment logs TWICE** â€” AUD-0007 had exactly 324 rows = 162 Ã— 2 (2 distinct `createdAt` timestamps; 162 unique `(productId,color,size,previousQty,newQty)` keys, each duplicated). Inventory itself was unaffected because the CASE statements SET absolute quantities (idempotent).
- **Fix** (`audit.controller.js` `approveAudit`): deleted the redundant legacy batch `$transaction` block â€” the interactive tx now performs the SQL + `createMany` + status update exactly once. Also added `cache.delPattern('pos:inventory:')` to the approval invalidation (previously only `warehouse:`/`products:` were cleared, so the `pos:inventory:<outlet>` / `pos:inventory:all-outlets-view` family could serve stale numbers for up to the 2-min TTL on the approving instance).
- **Data cleanup**: deleted the 162 duplicate `InventoryAdjustmentLog` rows for AUD-0007 (kept one row per real adjustment; these are audit log records, NOT inventory â€” no inventory data was touched). 324 â†’ 162.
- **Verification**: `node -e require` syntax OK; live API stock comparison 154 exact / 8 off / 0 missing with all 8 off-rows matched to post-approval `PosSaleItem` lines; adjustment logs now 162.

### Fixed This Session â€” Production Out Routing for Outlet Orders (Accept 403 + no "Send to Outlet" action)
- **Symptom**: On the PRODUCTION OUT profile, Production Out cards showed an Accept button; clicking it failed with "Failed to accept: Unauthorized". Completed production orders had no way to be sent back to their originating outlet (expected: `Send to Johar Town Outlet` / `Send to Jail Road Outlet` â†’ `OUTLET_RECEIVE` â†’ outlet My Tasks).
- **Root cause 1 â€” Accept 403**: `OrderCard.jsx:1547` PRODUCTION_ACCEPTANCE "Accept â†’ PRODUCTION" button calls `POST /api/orders/:id/route` (`manualRouteOrder`), but the route in `order.routes.js:140` authorized only `['STORE', 'STORE_EMPLOYEE', 'SUPER_ADMIN', 'ADMIN', 'FAISAL']` â€” the production roles were missing, so `auth.middleware.js` returned 403 `Unauthorized` for PRODUCTION/PRODUCTION_IN/PRODUCTION_OUT users.
- **Root cause 2 â€” no route button**: `OrderCard.jsx` PRODUCTION-stage block always rendered "Production Complete â†’ Coming From Production" (`STORE_RECEIVE`, the Faisal flow) regardless of `order.source`. Outlet orders (`source === 'OUTLET'`) need to go back to the originating outlet via `OUTLET_RECEIVE`. The backend `returnToOutlet` (`order.controller.js:3049`) already implements exactly that (validates `source === 'OUTLET'`, completes active stage, creates `OUTLET_RECEIVE` PENDING with deadline, assigns outlet users by `order.outletName` minus ` Branch`, routingHistory + audit + notify + `order-updated`) and is routed at `order.routes.js:155` with production roles authorized â€” it just was never exposed in the UI.
- **Fix 1 â€” backend** (`order.routes.js:140`): added `'PRODUCTION', 'PRODUCTION_IN', 'PRODUCTION_OUT'` to the `POST /:orderId/route` authorize list â€” production staff can now accept into production.
- **Fix 2 â€” frontend** (`OrderCard.jsx` PRODUCTION-stage block): when `order.source === 'OUTLET'`, primary button becomes **"Send to <originating outlet> Outlet â†’ OUTLET_RECEIVE"** (derived from `order.outletName`, `Branch` stripped) calling `POST /api/orders/:id/return-to-outlet` with confirm + toast + `onMarkSeen`; Faisal orders keep the original "Production Complete â†’ Coming From Production". Report Problem unchanged for both.

### Implemented This Session â€” Order Tracking (locate) + Order Control (manual transactional re-route) under Software Settings (commits `24a2b46` + `a631143`)
- **Requirement**: Add an **Order Tracking** module (locate an order's exact queue location by order # / invoice # / customer name) and an **Order Control** module (manual transactional re-route with reason) under **Software Settings**. Follow the established `softwareSettings.routes.js` convention (company-office check, profile bucket re-key, final summary find, terse comments, `console.error` logging). Re-route must be ONE atomic transaction: complete current stage, create new stage (PENDING, prevStage=source, destStage=target), reset destination-profile seen-tasks, write routing history + audit log, notify destination profile. Store/Logo non-admitted destinations always override to `PRODUCTION_ACCEPTANCE`. Terminal orders (cancelled/completed/rejected) cannot be re-routed.
- **Backend** (`backend/src/controllers/orderControl.controller.js` + `backend/src/routes/orderControl.routes.js`, mounted at `/api/order-control` in `backend/src/app.js`):
  - `locateOrder` (`GET /api/order-control/locate/:query`) — allows SUPER_ADMIN/ADMIN/SOFTWARE_SETTINGS; exact orderNumber/invoiceNumber match first, then a `contains` (case-insensitive) fallback over orderNumber/invoiceNumber/customerName/customerPhone; returns `{ order, timeline, location }` where `location` = `{ bucket: 'unseen'|'seen'|'cancelled'|'completed'|'delivered', queue }` with a role-appropriate profile bucket re-key (e.g. ORDER_ENTRY â†' Faisal / Outlet Order Entry; STORE â†' Store Keeper / Store Employee; LOGO_DESIGN â†' Logo Design; PRODUCTION/PRODUCTION_ACCEPTANCE â†' Production; DISPATCH â†' Dispatch; IN_DISPATCH â†' In Dispatch; ENAMELS_DELIVERY â†' Enamels Delivery Boy; OUTLET_RECEIVE â†' Come From Production), a `timeline` built from stages + routing history, and `profileDefs` for the frontend to label queues.
  - `rerouteOrder` (`POST /api/order-control/:orderId/reroute`) — ADMIN/SUPER_ADMIN only; validates the order exists and is not terminal; validates the destination stage; normalizes Store/Logo non-admitted destinations to `PRODUCTION_ACCEPTANCE`; runs the whole mutation (current active stage â†' COMPLETED, new stage PENDING with prevStage/destStage + deadline, `order.currentStage`/`status` update, routingHistory entry, destination-profile seen-task reset, `MANUAL_RE_ROUTE` audit log) inside one `prisma.$transaction`; then `notify.create` to the destination profile + `io` emit. Returns `{ message, nextStage, order, location }` (re-resolved via `locateOrder` logic).
- **Live 500 root cause fixed (commit `a631143`)**: `Order` has NO `routingHistory` back-relation (`RoutingHistory.orderId` is a plain indexed string) â€” the original `include: { routingHistory: ... }` failed Prisma validation at runtime (live locate returned 500 `{"message":"Error locating order"}`). Added an `ORDER_INCLUDES` constant (stages, auditLogs, createdBy) + `findOrderWithHistory()` helper that fetches routing history separately via `prisma.routingHistory.findMany({ where: { orderId }, orderBy: { createdAt: 'asc' } })` and attaches it; `rerouteOrder`'s post-tx re-fetch uses the same pattern. Also extended the contains fallback to `customerName` (insensitive) + `customerPhone` because OrderTrackPanel advertises "order #, invoice #, or customer name".
- **Frontend**: `OrderTrackPanel.jsx` (locate-by-query UI, bucket/queue + timeline display) and `OrderControlPanel.jsx` (locate + re-route form with destination select, mandatory reason textarea, Admin-only gate) wired as `order-track` (MapPin) and `order-control` (Navigation) tabs in `SoftwareSettings.jsx`.
- **Verification**: `node --check` + require-load OK; `npm run build` EXIT=0; commits pushed (`24a2b46`, `a631143`). Deployed `dpl_F4sBCM11Cf3cuutJQsk4bvpiNrjv` (`smart-production-v2-o7kq8gfi5-...`) + re-aliased `smart-production-v2.vercel.app`; live index 200 NO-SSO-INTERCEPT, deployed `SoftwareSettings-WjYB9J9w.js` contains `/api/order-control/locate` / `Order Tracking` / `Order Control` / reroute markers. **Live smoke (ADMIN)**: locate `50037` â†' 200 `#50037` Saima Iffat bucket=cancelled (timeline 7); locate `REP-49502` â†' 200 bucket=unseen Logo Design â€” Unseen Tasks; locate `Saima` â†' 200 `50349` Enamels Delivery Boy unseen (timeline 14); locate `JL-798585` â†' 200 Faisal / Outlet Order Entry unseen; invalid stage â†' 400; blank reason â†' 400. **Live E2E (disposable order, cleaned up)**: created `#OC-E2E-...` at ORDER_ENTRY â†' locate before = unseen Faisal/Outlet Order Entry â†' reroute â†' STORE = 200 (`Order re-routed to STORE`) â†' DB verified currentStage STORE / exactly one active `STORE:PENDING` stage / routingHistory `ORDER_ENTRY->STORE (Re-routed by Admin. Reason: E2E automated reroute verification)` / `MANUAL_RE_ROUTE` audit written / STORE seenTasks 0 after reset â†' rerouted back to ORDER_ENTRY (200) â†' all child rows + order deleted, leftovers 0. SSO protection left **disabled** (re-enabling re-intercepts all requests per documented deploy lesson). Untracked `backend/scripts/*.js` probe scripts intentionally not committed.

### Implemented This Session â€” Logo â†’ Production Now Goes Through Production In (Unseen Tasks filled, 31 live orders recovered) (commit `909efc2`)
- **Symptom**: Production In's Unseen Tasks tab was empty/missing orders even though 25+ ready orders existed; orders appeared to jump Logo â†’ Production without Production In ever seeing them.
- **Root cause (DB-proven)**: The Logo card's "Send to Production" button (`OrderCard.jsx:1605`) posted `{ nextStage: 'PRODUCTION' }` â€” writing a `PRODUCTION` stage row directly and **bypassing `PRODUCTION_ACCEPTANCE`** (Production In's only stage; `getRolesForStageBasedOnRole('PRODUCTION_IN') â†’ ['PRODUCTION_ACCEPTANCE']`, `PRODUCTION_OUT â†’ ['PRODUCTION','WORKERS']`). Histories read "Manually routed to PRODUCTION by Logo Deâ€¦". Live-DB probe: 116 active PRODUCTION orders â†’ 31 are `LOGO_DESIGN COMPLETED â†’ PRODUCTION PENDING` bypasses (the rest are `PRODUCTION IN_PROGRESS` already being worked by Production Out, or correctly gated). A second contributor: the interim `77956b5` tab deployment had marked the then-acceptance orders `seen`, and the new Unseen-only tab rendered only `unseen` â€” leaving them stranded.
- **Fix 1 â€” Logo button routes to the gate** (`OrderCard.jsx`): "Send to Production" now posts `nextStage: 'PRODUCTION_ACCEPTANCE'` (label â†’ PRODUCTION ACCEPTANCE). The order lands in Production In's Unseen Tasks, is accepted there, then routes to PRODUCTION for Production Out â€” exactly the intended workflow.
- **Fix 2 â€” backend normalization guard** (`order.controller.js` `requestStageCompletion`): if a Logo stage (`LOGO_DESIGN`/`NAME_LOGO`/`CUSTOM_LOGO`) completing its design requests `PRODUCTION`, the destination is normalized to `PRODUCTION_ACCEPTANCE` â€” legacy/cached clients can no longer bypass Production In even via direct API.
- **Fix 3 â€” manual route guard** (`order.controller.js` `manualRouteOrder`): same normalization for the `/route` endpoint (logo stage â†’ `PRODUCTION` â†’ `PRODUCTION_ACCEPTANCE`).
- **Fix 4 â€” Production In never strands seen orders** (`order.controller.js` `getUnseenOrders`): PRODUCTION_IN returns **every** acceptance-stage order in `unseen` regardless of `seenTask` (seen = []), so previously-marked-seen-but-not-accepted orders stay visible in the only tab they have. Once accepted the order routes to PRODUCTION and leaves this list (stage map excludes PRODUCTION) â€” no double-appearance, Production Out unchanged.
- **Data recovery (live, idempotent script since deleted)**: a one-time recovery classified the 117 active PRODUCTION orders by stage-row logic and moved the **31 true bypasses** (`PRODUCTION` PENDING, no preceding COMPLETED acceptance) back to a fresh `PRODUCTION_ACCEPTANCE PENDING` stage: completed the PRODUCTION stage rows, created one acceptance stage with a 24h deadline, set `currentStage='PRODUCTION_ACCEPTANCE'`/`status='PENDING'`, deleted stale `seenTask` rows at PRODUCTION/acceptance (so they leave Production Out's list and appear unseen in Production In), and wrote a `RECOVERED` audit log per order. **85 `IN_PROGRESS` orders were deliberately left untouched** (Production Out already working them). Verified: 36 orders now at `PRODUCTION_ACCEPTANCE` (5 pre-existing + 31 recovered), each with exactly 1 acceptance stage row, no stale seen-tasks.

### Implemented This Session â€” Production In/Out Task Visibility, Duplicate Stage Rows, and Continuous-Refresh Fix (commit `9a7330c`)
- **Requirement**: Production In must show ONLY the "Unseen Tasks" tab (the earlier `77956b5` change adding Assigned/Accepted for PRODUCTION_IN was wrong and reverted). Workflow: Outlet/Store â†’ Production In (Unseen â†’ Accept) â†’ Production Out (Assigned/Accepted â†’ Continue Production). Production Out must not bounce orders back to Production In on refresh/refetch. No full-page refresh, no polling loop, no flickering; counts must be real, exact, non-duplicated; completed orders excluded; an order must never appear in both In and Out at once. In â†’ Out routing logic untouched.
- **Frontend fix â€“ tabs + poll** (`MyTasks.jsx`): re-added `const isProductionIn = user?.role === 'PRODUCTION_IN'`; wrapped the Assigned/Accepted tab button (which renders only `unseenData?.seen`) in `{(!isProductionIn) && (...)}` so Production In sees only Unseen Tasks (default filter `unseen`); Unseen Tasks tab stays gated `(!isOutlet) && (!isProductionOut)` so Production Out keeps Assigned/Accepted only. **Removed the 30s `setInterval(() => scheduleRefresh(), 30000)` full-list poll entirely** â€” remaining refresh sources are mount, manual Refresh button, action-triggered `refreshTasks()`, and socket events (`order-verified`/`order-updated`/`notification:new` â†’ debounced `scheduleRefresh`; socket is a no-op stub on Vercel so no unintended production polling).
- **Frontend fix â€“ flicker** (`useCache.js`): new opt-in `sameData` predicate â€” when a refetched payload is semantically identical, it still refreshes hot cache + IndexedDB and clears loading but skips `setData`, so identical background polls no longer replace React state (no list/count flicker). Applied in `MyTasks.jsx` via `taskSignature = (list) => list.map(o => `${o.id}|${o.currentStage}|${o.status}`).join(',')` and `tasksSame = (a,b) => sig(a.unseen)===sig(b.unseen) && sig(a.seen)===sig(b.seen)`.
- **Backend fix â€“ duplicate stage rows** (`order.controller.js`): `manualRouteOrder` made idempotent/duplicate-safe â€” current stage completed via `orderStage.updateMany` (all PENDING/IN_PROGRESS/WAITING_APPROVAL rows, not just first `find`), destination stage created only when no active row exists for it (retried/double route reuses it). `getUnseenOrders` gains reader-side `dedupeOrderStages(stages)` (one row per stageName, preferring the active PENDING/IN_PROGRESS/WAITING_APPROVAL row over COMPLETED/stale copies) applied to both `unseen` and `seen` buckets â€” `OrderCard`'s first-match `currentStage` find (`OrderCard.jsx:19-20`) now always gets the active row even on orders with 2â€“13 duplicate stage rows (live DB had 8 such orders; one had 13 PENDING copies). No live DB mutation â€” duplicates are prevented going forward and neutralized in API responses.
- **Verification**: `node --check` on `order.controller.js` OK; `npm run build` EXIT=0 (3171 modules, only pre-existing POSPrint dynamic/static-import warning; new `useCache-ooSLM-G3.js` chunk). Commit `9a7330c` pushed (`77956b5..9a7330c`); deployed from repo root (first `frontend/` attempt failed exactly as the documented lesson: Vercel resolved the project root from `frontend/` so the root `vercel.json` install command's `cd backend` could not find the Prisma schema) â€” `vercel --prod --yes` â†’ `dpl_2FN843zSt8e1ZYqz4tWauvJWK4JA` (`smart-production-v2-g2n6t26q6-...`) READY (46s build) + auto-aliased project-scoped domain + re-aliased `smart-production-v2.vercel.app` (2s). Live checks: index 200, no `data-dpl-id` SSO intercept (`index-DqAG1TID.js`); deployed `MyTasks-DEwxJgXa.js` contains `sameData` + `Assigned/Accepted` + `Unseen Tasks` and **no** `setInterval(…30000)` poll.
- **Note**: live API probing still blocked by the device gate (all production users 401 "This device is not authorized"); end-to-end smoke must be done in an already-authorized browser session.
