### Implemented This Session â€” Cancelled Order Number Reuse Guard Closed for Legacy `#`-Prefixed Storage (commit `95b4d97`)
- **Requirement**: A cancelled order number must stay blocked from reuse forever. The live E2E harness (T7) exposed that `createOrder`'s reuse check (`findUnique({ orderNumber })`, order.controller.js:280) missed the legacy `#`-prefixed storage form (live DB stores numbers like `#50037`; the frontend submits bare `50037`), so a retry of a cancelled number could slip past and create a new order.
- **Fix** (`order.controller.js` `createOrder` manual-number branch): the reuse check is now `prisma.order.findFirst({ where: { OR: [{ orderNumber }, { orderNumber: `#${bareNumber}` }] }, select: { id: true, status: true } })` where `bareNumber = String(orderNumber).trim().replace(/^#/, '')` — matches BOTH the submitted form and the `#`-prefixed stored form, so a cancelled number is blocked whether typed with or without `#`. CANCELLED status still returns the dedicated `400 "This order number has already been cancelled and cannot be reused."`; any other existing status keeps the generic duplicate 400 + SECURITY_ALERT.
- **E2E harness completed (13/13 PASS)**: the temp `backend/scripts/cancel-e2e.js` was fixed (harness defects, not product bugs): mock `res.json` now defaults `_status` to 200 (Express behavior), and `ADMIN` uses the real SUPER_ADMIN id `57680058-746a-4698-973f-740a04f17a26` (the prior fake `admin-e2e` id violated the `decidedById` FK → approval `$transaction` rolled back → cascade of T4–T6 fails; the clean rollback itself proved approval atomicity). T6's assertion was also corrected — `trackOrder` returns the raw order object, not `{ order }`. Live run against Supabase: T1 blank reason 400 → T2 request PENDING + persisted → T3 lookup bare/`#` 200 → T4 approve → order CANCELLED + currentStage CANCELLED + stages REJECTED + request APPROVED → T5 lookup APPROVED → T6 trackOrder CANCELLED + cancellationRequest → **T7 cancelled-number reuse blocked 400 with the dedicated message** → T8 absent from active-queue filter. All disposable orders + child rows deleted (verified `leftovers: 0`). Harness deleted after use.
- **Verification**: `node --check` OK; `npm run build` EXIT=0; commit `95b4d97` pushed (`73dd0de..95b4d97`); deployed `dpl_4QFhAWqzNgRdF8WoZrqv1f7VQFub` (`smart-production-v2-64i90asda-...`) + re-aliased `smart-production-v2.vercel.app`; live index 200, no `data-dpl-id` SSO intercept.

- **Backend** (`audit.controller.js` + `audit.routes.js`):

  - `zeroItems` (`POST /:id/zero`, itemIds array or `{ all: true }` for unscanned) — single parametrized raw UPDATE `SET physicalQty=0, scanned=true, zeroed=true`, cap 3000 ids, persists summary, emits `audit-updated`; sets `zeroed` in `scanBarcode`/`batchScan`/`setPhysicalQty` (a barcode re-scan flips `zeroed` back to false and increments qty/scanCount).
  - `computeAuditSummary`/`persistSummary` carry all new fields; `listAudits` selects them + `_count.adjustments`; `submitAudit` persists; approve/reject set `endedAt` (approve also sets `approvedBy`/`approvedAt`, reject `rejectedBy`/`rejectedAt`/`rejectionReason`). `approveAudit` transaction re-verified — outlet CASE stock update, store variant JSONB+stock CASE, store flat stock CASE, single `createMany` adjustment log, `summary.appliedAdjustments`, 30s timeout, full cache invalidation (`warehouse:`/`products:`/`pos:inventory:`).
- **Frontend**:
  - `WarehouseAudit.jsx`: readiness precheck in the Start New Audit wizard (`runPrecheck` poll + amber blocked panel listing demands, green ready panel); `startAudit` blocked client-side on precheck failure; snapshot header is now 8 cards (Total Items, Snapshot Stock, Total Inventory Value, Scanned Items, Scanned Qty, Unscanned, Zeroed, Total Scans); new **Unscanned Item Analysis** panel (first 100 unscanned, per-row Zero + Zero All Unscanned with confirm); filter chips add Unscanned/Zeroed; zeroed rows show ABSENT badge; history table gains Scanned/Unscanned/Zeroed columns; print meta includes the new fields.
  - `AuditReview.jsx`: pending-approval (`SUBMITTED`) banner explaining the POS lock with open-detail buttons; history table Scanned/Unscanned/Zeroed columns + Decision column (approver/rejector + times); detail modal summary grid expanded (Scanned Qty, Unscanned, Zeroed, Missing, Extra, Inventory Value) + rejection metadata; ABSENT badges on zeroed item rows.
- **Verification**: `node -e require` OK (controller exports `getAuditPrecheck`, `zeroItems`; routes load); `npm run build` 0 errors from repo root (new `AuditReview-DfLBdom4.js` + `WarehouseAudit-DHd2sfSe.js` chunks); deployed `dpl_7FPSPvyLASpmLxcjuC8o48WDzoAh` (`smart-production-v2-44qv37s7z-sameerbutt056-1019s-projects.vercel.app`) + re-aliased `smart-production-v2.vercel.app`; live index `index-CBFZ1SA0.js` (no SSO intercept), `/api/audit/precheck` 401 unauthenticated (live + auth-guarded), deployed chunks contain `precheck`/`/zero`/`Unscanned`/`Zero All`/`scannedQty`/`unscannedCount`/`zeroedCount`/`ABSENT` markers.

### Implemented This Session â€” System-Wide Data Audit (single-source-of-truth reconciliation) + Demand Approval Atomicity Hardening
- **Requirement**: Complete system-wide data audit and end-to-end QA — every displayed number (POS History, Registers, Invoices, Admin Dashboard) must equal the sum of its detail records; audit workflow idempotent (approval never applies twice); demand deduction correct/atomic/refresh-safe; Store My Tasks green-ticked products all deduct without affecting cross-marked products; historical records accurate.

  - Stored Register summary vs live recompute MATCH for all 8 closed sessions: JT 118903/47750/65250/66700 and JR 43736/88954/114539/94125 (2026-08-05..08-08).
- **Demand deduction correctness `scripts/verify-demand-deductions.js` (read-only, all PASS)** — for every recent APPROVED/PARTIALLY_APPROVED demand, the `DEMAND_REQUEST_APPROVED`/`PARTIALLY_APPROVED` audit log contains the `warehouse stock deducted: <product>: <qty>` details, every deducted target variant exists in `InventoryItem`, and no variant stock is negative:

Now the PENDING re-check + status update + `deductWarehouseStock` loop + `auditLog.create` all run in ONE 30s interactive `$transaction` (rolls back on any failure, so a retry is always valid); `deductWarehouseStock` accepts an optional tx client (defaults to `prisma` for any other callers); race-safe (in-tx PENDING re-check closes two-concurrent-approval race); `DEMAND_NOT_PENDING` errors return 400 (not 500). Cache invalidation (`pos:`/`warehouse:`/`products:`), socket emit, and notify stay outside the transaction.

- **Verification**: `node -e require` OK on `outletDemand.controller.js`; all three audit scripts run clean against live Supabase with zero discrepancies; temp probe deleted after use. Backend-only change â€” no frontend build impact. Commit `NEXT` (pending) â€” deploy requires `vercel --prod --yes` from repo root + re-alias `smart-production-v2.vercel.app`.

### Implemented This Session — Replacement Workflow Gaps Closed (REPLACEMENT badge in Store cards + auto case-complete sync)
- **Requirement**: Finish the replacement-module verification so it matches the 10-point redesign spec — (a) REP- orders must be visibly identifiable as replacements in Store My Tasks, and (b) the ReturnExchange case status must auto-sync to `REPLACEMENT_COMPLETED` when the REP- order completes its pipeline (no manual Store action needed).
- **Gap (a) — REPLACEMENT badge** (`frontend/src/components/OrderCard.jsx`): added a blue **REPLACEMENT** badge (`RotateCcw` icon) in the header badge row right after the type badge, gated on `order.source === 'REPLACEMENT'` (`replacementCaseId` is always present on REP- orders, so source is the simplest reliable check). Previously replacement orders were only identifiable via the generic `Source:` field.
- **Gap (b) — auto case-complete sync** (`backend/src/controllers/order-helpers.js`): new exported helper `syncReplacementCaseOnOrderCompletion(order)` — no-op unless `order.replacementCaseId` is set; re-fetches the fresh order state and only proceeds when genuinely complete (`status === 'COMPLETED'` OR `currentStage === 'DELIVERED'` OR `deliveredAt` set, so stale pre-update objects from callers are handled); looks up the linked `ReturnExchange`; idempotent skip if `replacementCompleted` already true; otherwise in one `$transaction` sets `status: 'REPLACEMENT_COMPLETED'` + `replacementCompleted: true` + `replacementCompletedAt: now` + `replacementCompletedBy: 'System (replacement order completed)'`, and writes a `REPLACEMENT_STATUS_UPDATED` audit log on the case's original `orderId` with the REP- order number. Returns `{ synced, reason/caseId/updated }` and never throws.
- **Wired at all 6 genuine order-completion sites**: `order.controller.js` (requestStageCompletion OUT_FOR_DELIVERY final + approveStageCompletion final, after `calculateAndRecordRevenue`), `order-delivery.controller.js` `updateDeliveryStatus` DELIVERED branch, `delivery.controller.js` `deliverOrder`, `dispatch.controller.js` `markPickedUp`, `inDispatch.controller.js` `routeOrder` customerTakeDeliver. (Not hooked into `calculateAndRecordRevenue` itself because `createOrder` calls it for prepaid orders at creation — that is NOT a completion; and not into `deliverToOutlet`, which routes onward rather than completing.)

### Implemented This Session - Replacement Workflow Module (Faisal opens replacement from hub, loads original order, defines new product, sends to Store as a real REP- order)
- **Requirement**: The Faisal Replacements page becomes a workflow module: Faisal opens a replacement, loads the original order details automatically, defines the new replacement product, generates a summary, and sends it to Store. Store restocks the ORIGINAL returned goods and processes the NEW item through the existing per-product availability ticks (deduct if In Stock, route to Production/Logo if Not Available). The new product flows through the normal pipeline as a real order with routing/history/job sheet. Stay identifiable as a replacement (not a normal order).
- **Schema** (`backend/prisma/schema.prisma`): `Order` gained `replacementCaseId String?` (links the replacement order back to its ReturnExchange case); `ReturnExchange` gained `replacementOrderId String?`, `replacementSummary Json?`, `originalRestocked Boolean @default(false)` + `At`/`By`, `replacementCompleted Boolean @default(false)` + `At`/`By`; status comment extended with `STORE_RECEIVE`/`REPLACEMENT_COMPLETED`. `npx prisma validate` OK, `prisma db push` synced live (DB already in sync), `prisma generate` v6.19.3.

  - `updateStatus` (`POST /:id/update-status`): allows IN_PRODUCTION / STORE_RECEIVE / REPLACEMENT_COMPLETED / CANCELLED, stamps `replacementCompleted/At/By` on REPLACEMENT_COMPLETED, audit `REPLACEMENT_STATUS_UPDATED`.

### Implemented This Session â€” Warehouse Redesign: Demand Approval Fix, Store Verification Deduction, Production Log, Warehouse POS Removed
- **Requirement**: Fix the Demand Approval 500 (undefined `demand` variable in the notify message) + deduct warehouse stock at approval time only; restore the Store verification auto-deduction (Available âœ“ deducts inventory; Verify / Not Available routes to Production without deduction); extend `ProductionRecord` with production history metadata + add a read-only historical **Production Log**; then **remove Warehouse POS entirely** so the Warehouse stops being a sales module.
- **Demand approval fix** (`outletDemand.controller.js`):
  - `deductWarehouseStock(inventoryItemId, color, size, qty)` rewritten: returns the **actually deducted qty** (was boolean), matches variants case-insensitively (empty color/size = any variant), recomputes the item's total `stock` from `variants` after a variant-level deduction so dashboard/report totals stay consistent, and clamps the simple-stock branch with `Math.min` (never negative).
  - Approval handler now deducts `approvedQty` per item for `status !== 'REJECTED'` (APPROVED / PARTIALLY_APPROVED), writes a `warehouse stock deducted: ...` list into the audit-log details, invalidates caches (`cache.delPattern('pos:')`, `delPattern('warehouse:')`, `delPattern('products:')`), emits socket `inventory-updated`, and builds the notify + toast message from `existing.transferNumber` â€” **fixing the 500** (the old code referenced the unpopulated `demand` variable).
  - `acceptDemandRequest` no longer deducts warehouse stock (deduction moved to approval-only; acceptance just adds items to outlet inventory) â€” an intermediate edit briefly duplicated the deduction call and both were then removed; accept audit text now reads `accepted â€” N items added to outlet inventory`.
- **Store verification deduction restored** (`order.controller.js`): the STORE branch of `requestStageCompletion` (~:743) no longer just records availability â€” it persists per-index `availabilityStatus`, computes `availableIndices` (explicit `productAvailability` map wins; else honors persisted `not_available`/`produced` + `order.productVerification`; else all items), then calls `classifyOrderItems(order, availableItems)` + `deductInventoryItems(order, req.user.id, inventoryItems)` **for available items only**, wrapped in try/catch so stage routing proceeds even if deduction fails. `approveStageCompletion` (Verify path, :928) intentionally still does **no** deduction.
- **ProductionRecord extended** (`schema.prisma` ~:411): added `color String?`, `size String?`, `variantLabel String?`, `productionStatus String @default("COMPLETED")`, `productionEmployee String?`, `completedAt DateTime?`, `orderNumber String?` + indexes on `orderNumber`/`productionStatus`/`productionEmployee`. `npx prisma validate` OK; `npx prisma db push --skip-generate` applied live to Supabase; `npx prisma generate` (Prisma 6.19.3; 7.9.1 update notice only).
- **Production API** (`production.controller.js` + `production.routes.js`):
  - New `getProductionLog` â†’ `GET /api/production/log` â€” read-only historical log; filters `search` (OR across productName/orderNumber/orderId/productionEmployee), `status`, `startDate`/`endDate`, `limit` (default 200, max 500); backfills `orderNumber` for legacy records via one `prisma.order` fetch; returns `{ records, total }`. Route `router.get('/log', authenticate, getProductionLog)`.
  - `createProductionRecord` now accepts/persists color/size/variantLabel/productionStatus/productionEmployee/completedAt (auto-now when COMPLETED)/orderNumber; `getProductionRecords` select extended; export list includes `getProductionLog`.
  - Both `productionRecord.create` sites in `order.controller.js` enriched (the dead-code `createProductionRecordFromOrder` at :163, and the `notes: 'Added to inventory by Store'` site in the production add-to-inventory flow ~:1879) â€” now write orderNumber, variant fields, `productionStatus: 'COMPLETED'`, `productionEmployee`, `completedAt`.
- **Production Log tab** (`WarehouseDashboard.jsx`): `TABS` gained `'prod-log'`; tab button with ClipboardList icon; state `prodLogSearch`/`prodLogStatus` + `useCache('warehouse:production-log:${search}:${status}')` fetching `/api/production/log`; body with search input, status select (COMPLETED/IN_PROGRESS/CANCELLED/REJECTED), refresh button, and a table (Order #, Product, Variant, Qty, Status, Production Date, Completion Date, Employee). **`handleDemandApprove`** now also calls `refreshInventory()` + `refreshActiveTab()` after a successful approval so the deducted stock numbers update instantly without a manual reload.
- **Warehouse POS removed**: deleted `pages/WarehousePOS.jsx`, `context/WarehousePOSContext.jsx`, `components/WarehousePOSProducts.jsx`, `WarehousePOSCart.jsx`, `WarehousePOSModals.jsx`, `WarehousePOSHistory.jsx`, `WarehousePOSReturns.jsx`; removed the lazy import + `/warehouse-pos` route from `App.jsx`; removed the `Warehouse POS` nav item from `Layout.jsx` (ShoppingCart still used by Outlet POS); removed the three `/warehouse-pos` cards (POS Sales, Returns, Total Revenue) from `StoreDashboardPage.jsx` Complete Store Analytics grid. Backend `/api/warehouse/sales` etc. are **kept** because `WarehouseAnalyticsCard.jsx` (Admin Dashboard) still consumes `/api/warehouse/sales`; the audit module uses its own djb2/WRH scheme and does not depend on warehouse POS endpoints.
- **Verification**: `node -e require` OK on all 4 changed backend files; `npm run build` 0 errors (dist no longer emits a `WarehousePOS-*.js` chunk). ### Implemented This Session â€” Store Card Per-Product "Available âœ“" Tick for Inventory Deduction (Process & Route)
- **Requirement**: The Store My Tasks card must give the store keeper a per-product **tick option for inventory deduction** â€” the user reported "THERE IS NO OPTION OF TICK FOR INVANTORY DEDUCTION". Each product needs an explicit Available âœ“ / Not Available toggle; when the order is **Processed & Routed**, ticked items deduct from inventory while unticked items persist as `not_available` and flow to Production (no deduction). Mixed available/not-available items in one order must work independently.
- **Root cause**: The backend STORE branch of `requestStageCompletion` (`order.controller.js:759`) already supported an explicit per-index `productAvailability` map (`{ idx: bool }`) â€” persisting `availabilityStatus: 'available'/'not_available'` per item (order.controller.js:766), computing `availableIndices` from the map, and calling `classifyOrderItems` + `deductInventoryItems` for available items only. But the Store card UI only exposed the indigo **VER** toggle (`toggle-product-verification`, which gates the *fallback* deduction path) and the "Process & Route" button sent `{ inventoryStatus: 'Available', nextStage }` **without** `productAvailability` â€” so the explicit per-product map was never sent and there was no visible tick.
- **Fix 1 â€” per-product availability state** (`OrderCard.jsx`): New `availabilityTicks` state (a `Set` of product indices, defaulted to available unless the item's `productDetails` already has `availabilityStatus: 'not_available'`/`'produced'`); `handleAvailabilityToggle(idx)` toggles a single item; `buildProductAvailability(itemCount)` emits the `{ idx: bool }` map the backend expects.
- **Fix 2 â€” tick buttons** (`OrderCard.jsx` STORE render): each multi-item row (next to the VER button) and the single-item "Stock" row now show a **âœ“ IN STK** (emerald, "Available â€” will deduct from inventory") / **âœ— NO STK** (gray, "Not Available â€” no inventory deduction (goes to Production)") toggle. VER button untouched.
- **Fix 3 â€” Process & Route payload** (`OrderCard.jsx` :1560): the main process/route button now sends `productAvailability: buildProductAvailability(itemCount)` alongside `inventoryStatus: 'Available'` (count = `orderItems.length` for multi-item, `1` for single-item), so the backend persists each item's status, writes the `AVAILABILITY_UPDATED` audit, and deducts only ticked items. "Missing / Unavailable", Hold, Return, Reject paths unchanged.
- **Verification**: `npm run build` 0 errors (`built in 43.17s`); `node -e require` OK on `order.controller.js`/`order.routes.js` (backend unchanged). Commit `9e13c6c` pushed (`8594fca..9e13c6c`); deployed `smart-production-v2-7u1x5t73l-sameerbutt056-1019s-projects.vercel.app` (`dpl_CE1npCTABu8jgmnyBWsVzLvpj9ac`) + re-aliased `smart-production-v2.vercel.app`; live index `index-CM5aqgJ1.js` references `OrderCard-67l8E4eQ.js` which contains `IN STK` / `NO STK` / `productAvailability` markers.

- **Requirement**: Add `Unstitch â†’ Ø§ÙŽÙ† Ø³Ù¹Ú†` and `Contrast â†’ Ú©Ø§Ù†Ù¹Ø±Ø§Ø³Ù¹` to the Urdu dictionary and make them render correctly on the printed Job Sheet (previously shown in English).

### Implemented This Session â€” Demand Approval 500 Fix + Multi-Product Inventory Deduction (every ticked product deducts)
- **Requirement 1**: Store Module demand approval showed **"Error updating demand request"** even though warehouse stock WAS being deducted. Approval must succeed with no error, the UI must auto-refresh, and the demand status must update without a manual refresh.
- **Requirement 2**: Store My Tasks must deduct **every** green-ticked product â€” 2, 5, or 10 products all deducted â€” not just the first. Example: "Order contains 4 products. I mark all 4 with the green tick. Only the first product is deducted."
- **Root cause 1 â€” demand 500**: `outletDemand.controller.js` `approveDemandRequest` (:159-161) called `cache.delPattern('pos:').catch(() => {})`. `cache.delPattern` (`backend/src/utils/cache.js:54`) is **synchronous** and returns `undefined`; `.catch` on `undefined` throws `TypeError: Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'catch')`. The deduction loop runs **before** cache invalidation, so stock is deducted, then the TypeError bubbles to the catch â†’ `res.status(500).json({ message: 'Error updating demand request', error: error.message })` (:189), skipping the audit log, socket events, and success response. Reproduced: `c.delPattern('foo:').catch(...)` â†’ TypeError.
- **Root cause 2 â€” only-first-product deducted**: `deductInventoryItems` (`order.controller.js:1631`) looped per order item and built a **full `variants` array replacement** from the **same stale snapshot** (`[...inventoryItem.variants]`) for every item mapping to that inventory item. With multiple order items on the SAME inventory item (same product, different colors/sizes â€” e.g. order #50293: `BunFit Cap` Navy Palm + Floral; #50287: 2Ã— `Plain Surgical Caps` Black Emboos + Olive Emboos), each item pushed a complete `inventoryItem.update({ variants: <full array> })` into `$transaction([...])`. The operations run sequentially on the same row, so each later UPDATE **overwrote** the earlier one â€” only one product's deduction survived. (Real multi-item orders confirmed: #50302 2Ã— Sprinter Cap Unisex, #50281/#50273 2Ã— BunFit Cap, #50287 3 items, all mapping to the same inventory items.)
- **Fix 1 â€” demand approval** (`outletDemand.controller.js`): replaced the three `.catch(() => {})` calls with a `try { cache.delPattern('pos:'); cache.delPattern('warehouse:'); cache.delPattern('products:'); } catch (cacheErr) { console.error(...) }` block â€” `delPattern` cannot return a promise, so it is called bare inside try/catch. Stock deduction, audit log, `demand:updated` + `inventory-updated` socket events, and the 200 response now all complete. Frontend (`WarehouseDashboard.jsx` `handleDemandApprove` :339-355) already calls `fetchDemands()` + `refreshInventory()` + `refreshActiveTab()` on success, so UI auto-refresh + status sync are satisfied once the 200 is returned â€” no frontend change needed.
- **Fix 2 â€” multi-product deduction** (`order.controller.js` `deductInventoryItems` rewritten): groups all order items **per inventory item id** first, then for each inventory item applies **all** its deductions against **one evolving `updatedVariants` array** (find match â†’ check `available >= qty` â†’ decrement) and pushes a single `inventoryItem.update`. Audit logs now one per actually-deducted inventory item (summed qty). Verified with a simulation of the BunFit Cap shape: Navy Palm 10 + Floral 8 â†’ Navy Palm 9 + Floral 7, `newTotal` 27 â€” both colors deducted on the same item (old code: only one survived). Plain-stock path unchanged semantics (still `Math.min`-clamped decrement, now summed across deductions).
- **Verification**: `node -e require` OK on both controllers; `npm run build` 0 errors; node simulation proves both same-item variants deduct. Commit `3506d09` pushed (`3091a20..3506d09`); deployed `smart-production-v2-q0w53ne9v-sameerbutt056-1019s-projects.vercel.app` (`dpl_A7oi8VK8h1MUyJnwHQZaqaKvDTb3`) + re-aliased `smart-production-v2.vercel.app`; live health 200, index serves app (no SSO intercept).

### Implemented This Session â€” Per-Product Engraving Now Shows on Job Sheets Even When `skipEngraving` Is Stale (order #50175)
- **Symptom**: Order #50175 (`5cf21be6-4587-438d-b447-d478caec9514`, type READY_LOGO, at LOGO_DESIGN) has its per-product engraving details fully filled (item 2 `articleNames: ["DR FAROOQUE ALI","GENERAL SURGERY"]`, `nameSpelling`, `engravingType`, `namePrintingCharges: 300`) but they are NOT shown on the Job Sheet.
- **Root cause**: order-level `skipEngraving: true` (stale flag from entry defaults) suppressed the engraving section in all three renderers, even though per-product `customization` content was filled. Verified via DB script: order-level engraving fields empty, but item 2 `productDetails[1].customization` holds the real data. `skipEngraving` is unreliable â€” READY_LOGO orders can have it true while articleNames/nameSpelling/engravingType are filled.
- **Fix 1 â€” shared helpers** (`frontend/src/utils/engravingUtils.js`, new): `getFilledArticleNames(c)` (filters trim/non-empty article names) and `hasEngravingData(c)` (ignores `skipEngraving` entirely; true if engravingType/nameSpelling/nameColor/logoColor/logoPlacement/designNotes/designReference/logos/filled articleNames). Single source of truth for all three renderers.
- **Fix 2 â€” AllOrders.jsx**: Job Sheet engraving section gate (`hasCustomData` ~1055, both multi-item and single) now uses `hasEngravingData`; per-item Name Lines (~1466) and inline per-product spec (~1199) use `getFilledArticleNames` (parse string `customization`).
- **Fix 3 â€” OrderCard.jsx**: removed `skipEngraving` gates from Production Specs engraving, Name Lines, Logos, Special Notes (~403/450/468/483); Dispatch order-details engraving block (829) and custom-order block (~890) gated by `hasEngravingData`; verification-table articleNames/logos (~2338) and single-item "03. Engraving" + design-notes sections (~2559/2608) use `getFilledArticleNames` and a filled-logos filter (empty `[""]` / `{name:"",design:""}` entries no longer render blank lines).
- **Fix 4 â€” printReport.js**: `hasAnyCustomization` (1394) no longer returns false on `skipEngraving` â€” uses `hasEngravingData`; per-item `filteredNames` uses `getFilledArticleNames`. Printed Job Sheet now matches on-screen.
- **Verification**: `npm run build` 0 errors (only pre-existing POSPrint dynamic-import warning). Commit `8c81e30` pushed; `vercel --prod` deployment `dpl_HxEUjGNhKejHtHqgHkbo8UTDmfsn` (`smart-production-v2-5fb8w80a7-...`) aliased to `smart-production-v2.vercel.app`; live index serves `index-cPTKtjMI.js`; live chunks `OrderCard-CI1Yjmcy.js` + `printReport-CFuB0P4o.js` no longer contain the `skipEngraving` gate string.

Queue cards now show **Paid â‚¨â€¦ Â· Balance â‚¨â€¦ Â· POS reference** (or "No POS link") for at-a-glance verification. If no POS sale is linked, a note explains the order-number link requirement.

### Implemented This Session â€” Dispatch Slip Now Shows Real Product UNIT/TOTAL Prices (In Dispatch)
- **Symptom**: The In Dispatch Dispatch Slip printed the Products table with **UNIT â‚¨0 / TOTAL â‚¨0** for some orders (e.g. JT-692227) while the Financial Summary correctly showed TOTAL ORDER AMOUNT â‚¨5,650 / ADVANCE â‚¨1,999 / BALANCE â‚¨3,651.
- **Root cause**: Outlet orders' `productDetails` often store `unitPrice: 0` / no `totalPrice` â€” the real per-product price lives only on the linked POS sale (verified live: JT-692227 `order.totalPrice: 0`, item `unitPrice: 0`, but the linked `PosSaleItem` = `Crown Women / Navy / C / qty 1 / unit 5650 / line 5650`, `grandTotal 5650`). `slipProducts` in `InDispatch.jsx` derived UNIT/TOTAL exclusively from the order's `productDetails`, so it defaulted to â‚¨0.
- **Fix 1 â€” backend** (`inDispatch.controller.js` `getInDispatchOrders`): the linked `PosSale` select now also includes `items` (productName/color/size/quantity/unitPrice/lineTotal), and each returned order carries a new `_posItems` array â€” the exact per-product pricing from the original POS transaction.
- **Fix 2 â€” frontend** (`InDispatch.jsx` `slipProducts`): builds a name/color/size index from `order._posItems` and falls back to it for UNIT/TOTAL whenever the order's own `productDetails` price is 0 (matching by name+color+size, then name+color, then name). Orders with real `productDetails` prices keep them (fallback only kicks in when price is 0), so existing behavior is preserved.
- **Verification**: `node -e require` OK on controller; `npm run build` 0 errors (only pre-existing POSPrint dynamic-import warning). Commit `a3f3789` pushed; `vercel --prod` deployment `dpl_D7a52pmRu4VAVCfSBBC8R4ozDLFs` (`smart-production-v2-8yb274zp1-...`) aliased to `smart-production-v2.vercel.app`; live chunk `InDispatch-mxOGcZrY.js` confirmed to contain the `_posItems` fallback logic; live `/api/in-dispatch/orders` responds 401 unauthenticated (function live on the new build).

### Implemented This Session â€” In Dispatch â†’ Enamels Delivery Boy Orders Now Visible in Delivery Boy Profile
- **Symptom**: Orders routed from In Dispatch via **Enamels Delivery Boy** (`sendToEnamelsDelivery` â†’ `ENAMELS_DELIVERY` stage) did not appear anywhere in the Enamels Delivery Boy profile (`/delivery`, role `DELIVERY_BOY` â†’ `DeliveryDashboard.jsx`).
- **Root cause 1 â€” backend filter**: `routeOrder` in `inDispatch.controller.js` (and `outletOrder.controller.js` `sendToEnamelsDelivery`) only set `currentStage: 'ENAMELS_DELIVERY'` + `status: 'PENDING'` â€” it never tagged the order with `deliveryType: 'ENAMELS'` / `deliveryMethod: 'Enamels Delivery'`. `getDeliveryOrders` (delivery.controller.js:18) applies `where.AND = [{ OR: [{ deliveryType }, { deliveryMethod: methodStr }] }]` when `?deliveryType=ENAMELS` is passed, so untagged orders were excluded even though `currentStage: 'ENAMELS_DELIVERY'` is in the outer OR. The existing Dispatchâ†’Enamels path (`bookCourier` in dispatch.controller.js:163) DOES set both fields (that's why it worked).
- **Root cause 2 â€” frontend filter**: `DeliveryDashboard.jsx` fetcher's client filter kept only `OUT_FOR_DELIVERY` / `DELIVERED` / `status==='COMPLETED'` â€” `ENAMELS_DELIVERY`-stage orders were dropped even if the API returned them.
- **Fix 1 â€” backend tagging** (`inDispatch.controller.js` routeOrder + `outletOrder.controller.js` routeOrder): when `destinationStage === 'ENAMELS_DELIVERY'`, the order update now also sets `deliveryType: 'ENAMELS'` and `deliveryMethod: 'Enamels Delivery'` â€” matches the `bookCourier` convention, so reports/analytics/DispatchSheet see the correct method.
- **Fix 2 â€” backend robustness** (`delivery.controller.js` `getDeliveryOrders`): when `deliveryType === 'ENAMELS'`, the AND OR also matches `currentStage: 'ENAMELS_DELIVERY'` â€” legacy orders routed before the tagging fix still appear (only for ENAMELS; TCS/POST_EX queries unchanged).
- **Fix 3 â€” frontend filter** (`DeliveryDashboard.jsx`): client filter now keeps `currentStage === 'ENAMELS_DELIVERY'`. Such orders land in the **Pending** tab (`!riderAcceptedAt && not DELIVERED/COMPLETED`), are Acceptable via `/api/delivery/:id/accept`, and `deliverOrder`/`returnOrder` already complete the active `ENAMELS_DELIVERY` stage â€” no further workflow changes needed.
- **Verification**: `node -e require` OK on all 3 backend controllers; `npm run build` 0 errors. Commit `74aac25`.
- **Note**: `getDeliveryAnalytics` (EnamelsDeliveryCard) already handled untagged ENAMELS_DELIVERY orders via its 8-criteria OR â€” this fix brings the delivery-boy profile query and the routing writes in line with that.

### Implemented This Session â€” In Dispatch Control Point + Dispatch Slip + All Production Returns to Johar Town
- **Requirement**: Make In Dispatch the sole dispatch control point. Come From Production shows only **Send to In Dispatch**; the In Dispatch page owns routing (Enamels Delivery Boy / Customer Take / Send to Jail Road / Send to Johar Town) plus a per-order **Print Dispatch Slip** (payment info appears ONLY on the slip, never on the Production Job Sheet). Then make EVERY production-completed outlet order (Johar Town / Jail Road / Abbottabad) return to **Johar Town â†’ Come From Production** â€” JT is the central dispatch hub, no order auto-routes to its originating outlet.
- **Backend â€” `returnToOutlet`** (order.controller.js:3068): `targetOutlet` is now always `'Johar Town'` (was JR/ABâ†’JT but other origins kept originating-outlet routing). Standardized `returnReason = 'Returned to Johar Town from Production'` written to BOTH the completed active stage and the new OUTLET_RECEIVE stage (replaces old `'Returned to originating outlet from Production'`). routingHistory/seenTask/audit/notify all follow the `targetOutlet` variable â†’ now always Johar Town.
- **Backend â€” `requestStageCompletion`** (order.controller.js:789): the auto-route `PRODUCTION â†’ OUTLET_RECEIVE` branch (line 749, outlet orders) now ALSO tags the created stage with `returnReason: 'Returned to Johar Town from Production'` so that path is classified as a production return too (not just `returnToOutlet`).
- **Backend â€” `getComeFromProduction`** (outletOrder.controller.js:1125): Johar Town users now see **every** `OUTLET_RECEIVE` order (`outletName` filter removed â€” all production returns from any origin, incl. `General`); Jail Road / Abbottabad see only orders routed **to** them (e.g. from In Dispatch / delivery boy) by EXCLUDING production returns: active OUTLET_RECEIVE stage must have `returnReason: null` OR `not contains 'Production'`.
- **Frontend â€” `OrderCard.jsx`** (PRODUCTION block): the outlet button now reads **"Send to Johar Town Outlet â†’ OUTLET_RECEIVE"** unconditionally (was outletName-derived, only JR said Johar Town) and calls `return-to-outlet`.
- **Prisma gotcha found live**: `mode: 'insensitive'` inside a `not { contains }` filter is rejected at runtime (`Unknown argument 'mode'`, suggests `lte`) even though the generated type is `StringNullableFilter` and top-level `contains + mode` works. Fix: drop `mode` and use plain `contains: 'Production'` â€” the only `OUTLET_RECEIVE.returnReason` values are old `'Returned to originating outlet from Production'` and new `'Returned to Johar Town from Production'`, both capitalized, so case-sensitive match is sufficient.
- **Live verification**: live DB shows exactly 1 Jail Road order in CFP â€” **JL-906468** (`returnReason: 'Returned to originating outlet from Production'`, routingHistory remarks `Returned to Johar Town outlet from Production (originated Jail Road)` â€” it was already routed to JT by the old JRâ†’JT rule; the JR user saw it because the OLD CFP filter matched `outletName contains 'Jail Road'`). After deploy: **JT CFP = 3 orders (incl. JL-906468)**, **JR CFP = 0**. No data migration needed â€” the code fix moved it.
- **Deployments**: `a94410d` (all-returns-to-JT + CFP split) and `d883992` (mode-filter fix) pushed, deployed (`dpl_CZZKQX6H5u5ZNRXwrjLNFdPbC8Bc`), aliased `smart-production-v2.vercel.app`. Live API re-check with real JT/JR logins confirms JL-906468 in JT CFP (unseen 1) and absent from JR CFP.
- **Note**: JT CFP now also lists In Dispatchâ†’Jail Road / delivery-boy-returned orders (JT = hub, sees all OUTLET_RECEIVE). This is intentional; final routing is decided from the In Dispatch module.

### Fixed This Session â€” Production Out Profile Shows Only Accepted Tasks (Unseen Tasks tab removed)
- **Symptom**: The Production Out profile (Enamels â†’ Production â†’ Production Out / "Luxe" user) showed an "Unseen Tasks" tab with a count, and an accepted order like JT-692227 (routed `PRODUCTION_ACCEPTANCE â†’ PRODUCTION` by the Production Out user) sat in **Unseen** â€” searching it showed "No Assigned Tasks" because the profile landed on Assigned/Accepted which only renders `seen`. Requirement: **"in this profile it has to show only accepted task not unseen task"** â€” the Production Out profile must show ONLY Assigned/Accepted, no Unseen Tasks tab.
- **Root cause 1 (frontend)**: A prior fix (`75a60bd`) had *added* the Unseen Tasks tab for PRODUCTION_OUT (tab gate `(!isOutlet)` only) and defaulted `taskFilter='unseen'`. Accepted orders land in `unseen` (not `seen`) because routing into a stage never creates a `seenTask` for the routing user â€” `seen` = orders where the user has a `seenTask` matching `order.currentStage` (order.controller.js:2504-2505).
- **Root cause 2 (backend)**: `manualRouteOrder` completed the current stage, created the destination stage, and reset recipient seen-tasks, but never created a `seenTask` for the routing user at the destination â€” so any order a Production Out user accepted (routed PRODUCTION_ACCEPTANCE â†’ PRODUCTION) stayed "unseen" forever for that user.
- **Fix 1 â€” frontend** (`MyTasks.jsx`): Unseen Tasks tab button now gated `(!isOutlet) && (!isProductionOut)` â€” hidden for PRODUCTION_OUT; `taskFilter` defaults to `'assigned'` for PRODUCTION_OUT (was `'unseen'`). Assigned/Accepted remains the only tab for that profile (it renders `unseenData.seen`).
- **Fix 2 â€” backend** (`manualRouteOrder`, order.controller.js:2358): after the recipient `seenTask.deleteMany`, an upsert now creates a `seenTask` for the **routing user** at the destination stage â€” any order the user routes into a stage immediately lands in their Assigned/Accepted list. Generic for all roles; other recipient users still see it unseen until they mark-seen.
- **Data backfill**: created `seenTask` rows for the Production Out user (`5ddc9321-42dc-43a9-9164-2eb3f275a4d8`) across all 105 active orders at `PRODUCTION`/`PRODUCTION_ACCEPTANCE`/`WORKERS` â€” 11 previously-unseen orders now seen; verified **UNSEEN: 0, SEEN: 105** after backfill.
- **Verification**: `node -e require` OK on `order.controller.js`; `npm run build` 0 errors (new `MyTasks-8gRlMatK.js` chunk â€” unchanged hash, only tab-gate + default changed); commit `2e5f3e6` deployed + re-aliased `smart-production-v2.vercel.app`.
- **Note**: JT-692227 has already advanced past PRODUCTION (live `currentStage=IN_DISPATCH`, `status=PENDING`) â€” it moved on its own after the user's screenshot; the Production Out profile now shows all its production work under Assigned/Accepted.

### Fixed This Session â€” Checkout 500 on Large Carts (Prisma 5s Transaction Timeout)
- **Symptom**: Checkout worked for a few items but returned 500 "Failed to create sale" when many products were in the cart.
- **Root cause**: `createSale` in `pos.controller.js` used a bare `prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => â€¦)` â€” Prisma's default interactive-transaction timeout is **5000ms**. Each cart item = one stock `UPDATE` inside the transaction, and through the Supabase pooler every query carries ~400â€“800ms latency, so a 15-item cart already ran ~9s live. Reproduction: live `POST /api/pos/sales` with 15 items â†’ 500 with error `"Transaction API error: Transaction already closed: The timeout for this transaction was 5000 ms, however 5186 ms passed"`.
- **Fix**: Raised the timeout to **`{ timeout: 30000 }`** (same pattern already used by the audit module for identical pooler latency) on all four POS transaction sites: `pos.controller.js` `createSale` + `createReturn`, `warehouse.controller.js` `createSale` + `createReturn`. `vercel.json` already sets `functions.maxDuration: 30`, so 30s is within the function limit.
- **Verification**: local 8/15/25-item carts all 201; live 15-item checkout now **201 in 12s** (was 500); live 25-item checkout **201 in 16.8s**; test sales deleted + stock restored after each run; `node -e require` syntax OK; commit `9eb4d3f` deployed + re-aliased `smart-production-v2.vercel.app` (deployment `npggpg0zw`).
- **Note**: 14 of 17 interactive `$transaction` calls across controllers still use the 5s default (outlet order, verification, order, returnExchange, inventory) â€” they haven't caused issues yet but could hit the same wall if their per-call work grows; audit + inventory ones already have explicit timeouts.

### Fixed This Session â€” Enamels Delivery Boy Analytics Empty (backend 500s) + Jail Road Orders Option
- **Symptom**: Admin Dashboard â†’ Enamels Delivery tab showed all-zero stats even though 88 Enamels-related orders exist. The card's `safeGet` swallowed errors silently.
- **Root cause**: `Order` model has **no `riderName` field**. `getDeliveryEmployeeStats` (~L612) and `getActivityTimeline` (~L803) selected `Order.riderName` â†’ Prisma threw `Unknown field 'riderName' for select statement on model 'Order'` â†’ both endpoints returned 500 on every call. Verified at runtime, not inferred.
- **Fix 1 â€” `getDeliveryOrders`** (`delivery.controller.js`): where clause now also matches `currentStage: 'ENAMELS_DELIVERY'` and statuses `RETURNED`/`CANCELLED`; added includes `orderAcceptances`, `deliveryChargeRecords`, `returnExchangeCases`.
- **Fix 2 â€” rider derivation**: `getDeliveryEmployeeStats` and `getActivityTimeline` no longer select `Order.riderName`; rider name is derived from `orderAcceptances[0].riderName` â†’ `deliveryAttempts` last `riderName` â†’ `deliveryPayments[0].collectedBy` â†’ `deliveryChargeRecords[0].riderName` (all real fields). Both endpoints no longer 500.
- **Fix 3 â€” `getDeliveryAnalytics`** (new, `GET /api/delivery/analytics`, auth): single authoritative endpoint replacing the card's 6-endpoint aggregator. Response `{ orders, stats, earnings, riders }`:
  - Order identity = 8-criteria OR (`deliveryType='ENAMELS'`, `deliveryMethod='Enamels Delivery'`, `currentStage='ENAMELS_DELIVERY'`, or existence of `deliveryAttempts`/`deliveryPayments`/`deliveryChargeRecords`/`orderAcceptances`/`noResponseLogs`) â€” needed because `outletRouteOrder.sendToEnamelsDelivery` sets `currentStage='ENAMELS_DELIVERY'` without `deliveryType`.
  - Per-order enrichment: derived `riderName`, `primaryStatus` (delivered/returned/cancelled/failed/noResponse/inTransit/pending from attempts + `deliveredAt`/`returnedAt`/`noResponseCount`/`status`/`currentStage`), `timeline` (assignedAt, acceptedAt, pickedUpAt, deliveredAt, returnedAt, noResponseAt, durationMinutes), `payments[]`, `deliveryCharge`, `attempts[]`, `cashCollected`/`onlineCollected`/`outstanding`/`isPaid` (COD = `totalPrice âˆ’ advanceAmount âˆ’ collected`).
  - `stats`: totalAssigned, accepted, pickedUp, delivered, pending, inTransit, returned, noResponse, cancelled, failed + totalOrderValue, codOrderCount, paidOrderCount, totalCOD, totalPaidAmount, outstandingCollection, cashCollected, onlinePrepaid.
  - `earnings`: totalEarnings, totalPaid, outstandingEarnings, completedDeliveries, perRider (per-order charge breakdown). Amounts from actual `DeliveryCharge` records (`amount || 200` fallback), paid vs outstanding via `isPaid`.
  - Filters: `dateFrom/dateTo` (OR across createdAt/deliveredAt/riderAcceptedAt/returnedAt/lastDeliveryAttempt), `riderName` (contains, applied AFTER riders list is built so dropdown stays populated), `status` (orderStatus OR orderStage), `deliveryStatus` (primaryStatus), `paymentType`, `outlet` ('jail road' â†’ only Jail Road outlet orders).
- **Frontend** (`EnamelsDeliveryCard.jsx`): full rewrite â€” single analytics fetch (30s polling + socket `order-updated`/`delivery-updated` refresh), date presets incl. custom range, rider/order-status/delivery-status/payment filter selects, **Jail Road toggle** (`outlet=jail road`), clickable stat chips with inline order lists, payment breakdown + outstanding lists, per-rider earnings cards with Pay/Payment-History modals, timeline table (cap 100 rows) + order detail modal.
- **Live verification** (mock req/res against Supabase, read-only): All Time = 88 assigned / 78 delivered / 4 in transit / 6 returned, â‚¨359,049 order value, â‚¨40,050 COD (13 orders), â‚¨237,299 cash, â‚¨58,850 online, earnings â‚¨14,800 (74 Ã— â‚¨200); Jail Road Only = exactly 3 JL orders (JL-188352, JL-805573, JL-631580), â‚¨600 earnings; Delivered Only / Cash Only consistent subsets. `npm run build` 0 errors; `node -e require` OK (17 exports).

### Implemented This Session â€” POS History Multi-Search (Invoice # / Customer Name / Phone, Whole DB)
- **Requirement**: The POS â†’ History search bar must find any invoice in the entire POS database by invoice number, customer name (partial), or customer phone number (partial), instantly and regardless of age (today â†’ 365+ days old). Existing receipt/invoice-number search must keep working.
- **Backend** (`pos.controller.js` `getSales`):
  - When a `search` query param is present, the date/range filter is **skipped entirely** â€” the query searches the whole `PosSale` table (not just the selected date window).
  - `where.OR` matches: `receiptNumber` (insensitive), `customerName` (insensitive), `customerPhone` (contains), `orderNumber` (insensitive), plus `orderId IN (...)` for order-linked sales whose `Order.invoiceNumber`/`Order.orderNumber` match (INV-â€¦, JT-â€¦).
  - **Search priority ordering**: exact invoice/receipt/order/phone number match (score 0) â†’ prefix match of a number (score 1) â†’ customer-name match (score 2), then by `createdAt` desc within each score.
  - Cache key normalized for search mode (drops redundant date/range segments).
- **Frontend** (`POSContext.jsx`):
  - New state `historySearchResults`/`historySearchLoading` (reducer keys + exports).
  - Debounced (350ms) server search effect: on `receiptSearch` change â†’ `GET /api/pos/sales?outlet=X&search=Q` (no range), race-guarded with `historySearchRef` so only the latest query updates results; clears results when input is empty.
  - `filteredSales` reworked: empty search â†’ range-filtered `sales`; non-empty search â†’ instant client-side multi-field filter over the loaded list (receipt/invoice/order number + name + phone) while the whole-DB server results are pending, then swaps to authoritative server results once they land.
  - `downloadExcel` now exports the search-filtered list (`filteredSales`) so a search + Excel export is consistent.
- **Frontend** (`POSHistory.jsx`): placeholder updated to "Search invoice #, customer name, or phone..."; pulsing "Searchingâ€¦" indicator while the debounced query is in flight; empty state "No invoices match your search"; customer phone now shown next to the name on each card.
- **Verification**: `node -e require` OK on `pos.controller.js`; `npm run build` exit 0.

### Implemented This Session â€” Inventory Audit High-Speed Barcode Scanning (POS-level performance)
- **Requirement**: The Audit scanner must feel as fast as the POS barcode scanner â€” instant per-scan feedback, continuous scan-â†’+1-â†’scan flow with no waiting, no full-page/table reloads, keyboard-free operation, and practical for thousands of products.
- **Frontend** (`frontend/src/components/WarehouseAudit.jsx`):
  - **In-memory barcode lookup**: `barcodeMapRef` (`Map<barcodeâ†’itemId>`) built once per audit open (`buildBarcodeMap` in `openAudit`/`startAudit`) â€” scanning is now a pure `Map.get()`, no server round-trip for recognition. Barcodes never change during an audit, so the map stays valid.
  - **Instant local increment**: `handleScan` is now synchronous â€” on match it reads the freshest item from `itemByIdRef` (in-memory mirror kept in sync after every commit), `+1`s it via a functional `setActiveAudit`, recomputes the summary locally (`computeLocalSummary`, mirrors backend), pushes to the Live Scanning Summary, captures `lastScannedRef`, clears the barcode field, and refocuses `scanRef`. Only that item's row is patched; the full audit is never re-fetched per scan. `VariantRow` is `React.memo`-ized and all row callbacks are `useCallback`-stable (via refs) so only the scanned row re-renders.
  - **Background batched sync**: scans AND manual-qty sets accumulate as ordered ops in `pendingOpsRef` (`{ op: 'scan'|'set', itemId, qty }`) and flush to `/api/audit/:id/batch-scan` â€” debounced 300ms, or immediately at 30 ops. `flushOps` drains in a self-rescheduling loop, requeues on failure, and the UI never awaits a DB write. `pendingCount` shows an amber "Syncing N op(s)â€¦" indicator.
  - **Live Scanning Summary panel**: newest-first deduped list of the last 50 scanned items â€” Product Name, Color, Size, Barcode, Scan Time, Times Scanned (from `scanCount`). Seeded from persisted `lastScannedAt`/`scanCount` on reload.
  - **Comparison table**: new Variant column, new Scans column (purple `scanCount`), filter chips (All / Scanned / Diff > 0), manual qty with âˆ’/+ buttons and a typeable input that commits on blur/Enter (`commitQty` no-ops when unchanged).
  - **Real-time totals**: header now shows Total Scans card alongside Total Variants / Snapshot Stock / Scanned / Remaining; summary recomputed locally on every patch.
  - **Invalid barcode**: `beep(false)` (WebAudio buzz) + toast "Barcode not found." + immediate clear + refocus â€” scanning continues uninterrupted. Matched scans get a subtle `beep(true)` tick.
  - **Focus recovery**: window `keydown` Enter handler refocuses the barcode field when focus is lost to a non-interactive element â€” no mouse/keyboard needed between scans.
  - **Submit integrity**: `submitAudit` calls `forceFlush()` (retry loop) to drain the queue, then posts authoritative absolute `finalCounts` so the exact scanned state lands in the DB even if a background batch was dropped. `goBack` flushes + guards the in-flight-flush edge.
- **Backend** (`backend/src/controllers/audit.controller.js`):
  - **`batchScan`** (`POST /api/audit/:id/batch-scan`, STORE/STORE_EMPLOYEE): accepts ordered `{ ops: [{ op: 'scan'|'set', itemId, qty }] }` (â‰¤300) OR legacy `{ scans: [{ itemId }] }`. Ops are **replayed per item in order** â€” a `set` fixes the absolute count, later `scan`s add on top â€” then each item is written **once** (aggregated) in a single 30s `$transaction`. Scans also increment `scanCount` and stamp `lastScannedAt`. Returns `{ processed, notFound, summary, synced, item }` â€” never the full item list.
  - **`submitAudit`**: accepts optional `{ finalCounts }` â€” applies absolute physical quantities as a **single parametrized SQL statement** (`CASE "id"::text WHEN ...`, one round trip â€” see "Fixed This Session" below), then recomputes + persists the summary (now including `totalScans`) from ONE items fetch and returns `{ ...updated, items, adjustments: [] }`.
  - **`scanBarcode`** (single) also increments `scanCount` + sets `lastScannedAt`. `InventoryAudit` gained a `totalScans Int @default(0)` column; `InventoryAuditItem` gained `scanCount` + `lastScannedAt` (pushed + regenerated). `approveAudit` wraps its per-item live-inventory writes in a 30s `$transaction`.
  - Route added in `audit.routes.js`; `persistSummary` refactored to accept a transaction client (existing `/scan` and `/items/:itemId` callers unchanged).
- **Verification**: `node -e require` OK on controller/routes; `npm run build` 0 errors (new `WarehouseAudit-DQ3JUZk7.js` chunk).

### Fixed This Session â€” "Error submitting audit" 500 (large audits exceeded Prisma's 5s transaction / Vercel function cap)
- **Symptom**: Warehouse â†’ Inventory Audit â†’ Submit Audit returned `500` â€” `api/audit/:id/submit` failed with `FUNCTION_INVOCATION_TIMEOUT` (Vercel) or a Prisma transaction error. Live test audit had 1598 items / 182 scanned.
- **Root cause 1 â€” N sequential UPDATEs through the Supabase pooler**: `submitAudit` applied `finalCounts` as `for (const id of ids) tx.inventoryAuditItem.update(...)` inside `prisma.$transaction` with **no timeout** (Prisma's 5s default). Every query costs ~0.5â€“0.8s over the pooler, so 180+ updates blew 5s instantly; even `prisma.$transaction([...updates], { timeout: 30000 })` (batch form) still executes serially over the pooler and exceeded Vercel's 30s function cap (hit 60.7s).
- **Root cause 2 â€” double fetch**: `submitAudit` fetched all items for the summary, then called `loadAuditWithItems` again for the response â€” 2Ã—1598-row fetches added seconds.
- **Fix**: apply `finalCounts` as **one parametrized SQL statement** via `Prisma.sql`:
  ```sql
  UPDATE "InventoryAuditItem"
  SET "physicalQty" = CASE "id"::text ${Prisma.join(whens,' ')} ELSE "physicalQty" END,
      "scanned" = true, "updatedAt" = now()
  WHERE "id"::text IN (${Prisma.join(idList, ',')}) AND "auditId"::text = ${audit.id}
  ```
  One round trip regardless of item count; response built from the single items fetch (`{ ...updated, items, adjustments: [] }`). Also bumped `vercel.json` `functions.maxDuration` 30 â†’ 60, wrapped `approveAudit`'s per-item inventory writes in a 30s transaction, and persisted `totalScans` in `computeAuditSummary`/`persistSummary`.
- **Gotcha discovered live**: Prisma binds JS-string params as **TEXT**, so `WHERE "id" = $1::uuid` still throws `operator does not exist: text = uuid` â€” the uuid column must be cast to text (`"id"::text = $1`), NOT the parameter. Verified with local no-op runs against the same Supabase DB (`::uuid` param fails, `::text` column cast succeeds).
- **Verification**: clean WAREHOUSE end-to-end â€” start audit (1692 items) â†’ ordered batch-scan (scan,scan,set3,scan â†’ it1 qty2/scans2, it2 qty4/scans1, totalScans3) â†’ submit with 2-item finalCounts â†’ **200 `SUBMITTED` in 6.2s** (was 60.7s timeout). Test audits AUD-0004/0006 rejected afterwards (no inventory touched). Commits `2f10122`, `e9f6ef6`, `9a7c849`, `0ecbc53`, `60ce846`, `b055899`, `582e03c` pushed + deployed + re-aliased `smart-production-v2.vercel.app`.

### Fixed This Session â€” "Failed to approve audit" 500 (same pooler wall as submit, but on Admin approve)
- **Symptom**: Admin Review â†’ Approve on the user's Johar Town audit returned **"Failed to approve audit"**. Reproduced live: `POST /api/audit/b9333ba4â€¦/approve` (AUD-0007, 385 scanned, 162 changed) failed after **33.1s** with `Transaction API error: Transaction not found. Transaction ID is invalid, refers to an old closed transaction` â€” the 30s interactive-transaction timeout. Same root cause as submit: `approveAudit` ran ONE `tx.outletInventory.update()` per changed item (162 UPDATEs through the Supabase pooler at ~0.5â€“0.8s each = way over 30s AND Vercel's 60s cap).
- **Fix** (`audit.controller.js` `approveAudit`): build ALL adjustments + in-memory inventory targets first (NO per-item DB writes), then apply inventory via **one raw SQL statement per table** inside a single fast interactive transaction:
  - Outlet items: `UPDATE "OutletInventory" SET "stock" = CASE "id"::text WHEN ${id} THEN ${qty} â€¦ ELSE "stock" END WHERE "id"::text IN (â€¦)` (same text-cast pattern as submit).
  - Warehouse items: group per `productId` FIRST (CASE returns the first matching WHEN, so duplicate product entries would win) â€” all variant edits for one product accumulate onto the SAME `variants` array, then one `UPDATE "InventoryItem" SET "variants" = CASE "id"::text â€¦ THEN ${JSON.stringify(variants)}::jsonb â€¦, "stock" = CASE â€¦ END` (+ a flat stock-only statement for products with no variants).
  - Same transaction also flips statusâ†’APPROVED, `createMany`s the adjustment logs, and stamps `appliedAdjustments` â€” 2â€“4 round trips total, atomic.
- **Verification**: local rollback tests against Supabase (outlet CASE 531ms, item jsonb+stock CASE 496ms, both SQL VALID), then live **approve AUD-0007 â†’ `APPROVED` in 10.5s, 162 adjustments applied**. Commit `a8dd22c` deployed + re-aliased `smart-production-v2.vercel.app`.

- **Requirement**: A branch's POS must NOT open/create sales while it has an inventory audit in `SUBMITTED` status (awaiting Admin approve/reject). Lock applies ONLY to the branch with the pending audit (Johar Town pending â†’ JT POS locked, Jail Road/Abbottabad unaffected). POS auto-unlocks the moment Admin approves OR rejects â€” no manual intervention.

Locked â†’ full-screen lock screen (Lock icon, the exact message, audit-number badge with branch/warehouse label, auto-unlock note). **Fail-open** on lock-check network error so a transient failure never takes the branch down (backend 423 remains the enforcement).
  - `OutletPOS.jsx`: wrapped in `<PosAuditLock type="OUTLET" outletName={selectedOutlet}>` (selectedOutlet from POSContext = the branch for OUTLET users; admins switching branches re-check). `WarehousePOS.jsx`: wrapped in `<PosAuditLock type="WAREHOUSE">`.
- **Live verification** (read-only except test audit, rejected after): baseline all unlocked â†’ created WAREHOUSE audit AUD-0008 (1692 items) + submitted â†’ `pos-lock?type=WAREHOUSE` = **locked=True AUD-0008**, `pos-lock?type=OUTLET&outletName=Johar Town` = **locked=False** (branch isolation), `POST /api/warehouse/sales` = **423** with the exact message â†’ Admin rejected AUD-0008 â†’ warehouse **locked=False** (auto-unlock). ### Fixed This Session â€” Outlet POS Barcode Scanner: Auto-Clear + Refocus for Continuous Scanning

- **Fix 1** (`POSContext.jsx` `handleBarcodeLookup`): Every exit path now clears the input and refocuses `barcodeRef` â€” success ("added via barcode"), invalid barcode (`Barcode not found` error), and not-logged-in (`Please login employee first`). Empty code path just refocuses.
- **Fix 2** (`POSContext.jsx` Enter handler): Window keydown Enter handler now only triggers when `document.activeElement === barcodeRef.current` â€” Enter pressed in the Search (or any other) field no longer fires a barcode lookup.
- **Fix 3** (`OutletPOS.jsx` barcode input): Added `autoFocus` so the cursor lands in the barcode field when the POS tab mounts, and returns there after every scan.
- **Result**: Cashier workflow is now **Scan â†’ Add to Cart/Increase Qty â†’ Input Clears â†’ Cursor Ready** with no keyboard/mouse interaction; duplicate scans increment quantity via the existing `existing.qty + 1` path.
- **Verification**: `npm run build` 0 errors (only pre-existing POSPrint dynamic-import warning); new chunk `OutletPOS-lnOza70H.js`.

### Implemented This Session â€” Inventory Audit Module (Warehouse + Outlet)
- **Requirement**: New **Inventory Audit** module. Only the **Warehouse profile** performs audits (STORE/STORE_EMPLOYEE â€” no Outlet users). Admin dashboard gets an **Inventory Audit** card linking to an Admin-only review page with pending/approved/rejected counts, totals, last audit, and highest-difference products. Full lifecycle: read-only snapshot on start â†’ barcode scanning with live progress â†’ submit â†’ Admin approve/reject â†’ automatic inventory updates + adjustment logs on approval.

Tables pushed via `prisma db push`.
- **Backend** (`audit.controller.js` + `audit.routes.js` mounted at `/api/audit` in `app.js`):
  - `GET /api/audit/stats` â€” dashboard cards (pending/approved/rejected/in-progress, totalAdjustments, lossValue, extraValue, lastAudit, highestDifferenceProducts top 5, last warehouse/outlet status).
  - `POST /api/audit` â€” start audit; one IN_PROGRESS/SUBMITTED audit per scope at a time; warehouse snapshot iterates `InventoryItem.variants` JSON (one row per color/size with **computed `WRH` barcode** reusing the exact djb2/generateBarcode algorithm from `warehouse.controller.js`, fallback to a single row per item when no variants); outlet snapshot reads `OutletInventory` rows (their persisted barcodes). Interactive `$transaction` with `{ timeout: 30000 }` (Supabase pooler latency exceeded Prisma's 5s default).
  - `POST /api/audit/:id/scan` â€” case-insensitive barcode (or `itemId`) â†’ physicalQty +1, marks scanned, recomputes summary; `POST /api/audit/:id/items/:itemId` â€” manual physical qty override (clamped â‰¥0) for unscannable barcodes.
  - `POST /api/audit/:id/submit` â€” read-only hand-off to Admin.
  - `POST /api/audit/:id/approve` (SUPER_ADMIN/ADMIN only) â€” applies quantity changes to live inventory (outlet `stock`; warehouse top-level `InventoryItem.stock` or matching variant stock in `variants` JSON, recomputing top-level total from variants), writes `InventoryAdjustmentLog` rows, invalidates `warehouse:`/`products:` cache families, emits `audit-updated` + `inventory-updated` socket events. `POST /api/audit/:id/reject` â€” Admin only, optional reason.
  - Role gating: start/scan/items/submit = STORE/STORE_EMPLOYEE; stats/list/get = STORE/STORE_EMPLOYEE/SUPER_ADMIN/ADMIN; approve/reject = SUPER_ADMIN/ADMIN only. OUTLET users blocked from all audit endpoints.
- **Frontend** (`frontend/src/components/WarehouseAudit.jsx` wired as an **Audit tab** in `WarehouseDashboard.jsx`): history view (stats cards + audit table), Start New Audit wizard (Warehouse/Outlet + outlet picker + notes), live active-audit view (snapshot totals, progress bar, matched/missing/extra/diff-value chips, barcode scan form with last-scanned feedback, searchable variant-level comparison table with system/physical/diff + manual âˆ’/+/qty inputs), Submit confirmation, read-only detail view, A4 print report, approved-audit adjustments table. New `/audit` route (lazy) + nav item `Inventory Audit` for STORE/STORE_EMPLOYEE.
- **Admin side** (`frontend/src/pages/AuditReview.jsx` at `/audit-review`): stats cards, status-filterable audit list, review modal (scanned items grid with system/physical/diff), Approve (auto-applies adjustments) / Reject (with optional reason). `AdminDashboard.jsx` module-card grid gained an `Inventory Audit` card (`path: '/audit-review'` â†’ navigates instead of switching tabs). Nav item `Audit Review` for SUPER_ADMIN/ADMIN.
- **No-live-impact guarantee**: audit items persist as their own snapshot rows; scanning/qty/submit never read or write live `InventoryItem`/`OutletInventory` stock. Inventory mutates only inside the approval `$transaction`. Barcodes/product IDs/variant IDs are never modified â€” only quantities.
- **Verification**: `npx prisma generate` required (db push alone does NOT regenerate the client â€” `prisma.inventoryAudit` was undefined until regenerate). Live smoke test (start OUTLET Johar Town â†’ scan barcode Ã—2 â†’ detail â†’ submit â†’ outlet/store blocked 403 â†’ admin reject) all passed; the test audit was REJECTED so no inventory changed. `npm run build` 0 errors; new chunks `WarehouseAudit-CEgXt2Xu.js` + `AuditReview-CYrwP5Zy.js`.
- **Known behavior**: differenceValue on a fresh audit shows total loss value because physicalQty starts at 0 for all unscanned rows (every unscanned variant counts as missing) â€” it converges to the real figure as items are scanned; users are expected to scan every variant before submitting.

### Fixed This Session â€” Outlet Routing Buttons Not Showing After Acceptance
- **Root cause**: `OrderCard.jsx` line 1864 had `['ORDER_ENTRY', 'OUTLET'].includes(currentStage?.stageName) ? (dispatch UI) : (OUT_FOR_DELIVERY / ORDER_ENTRY+OUTLET / OUTLET_RECEIVE / fallback)`. The outlet routing buttons were in the **ELSE** branch of this ternary, meaning when `currentStage.stageName === 'ORDER_ENTRY'` the TRUE branch (dispatch UI) would render and the ELSE branch (containing the outlet routing buttons at line 2022) was never reached.
- **Fix**: Restructured condition at line 1864 to `['ORDER_ENTRY', 'OUTLET'].includes(stageName) ? userRole === 'OUTLET' ? (outlet routing) : (dispatch UI) : (OUT_FOR_DELIVERY / OUTLET_RECEIVE / fallback)`. Now when outlet users see an ORDER_ENTRY stage order, routing buttons render directly instead of the dispatch UI.
- **IsJoharTown guards removed**: `{isJoharTown &&}` removed from Send to Logo, Send to Production, and Delivery Boy buttons â€” all outlets now see the full set of routing options (Send to Logo, Send to Production, Delivery Boy, Send to JT/Outlet, Customer Take). This was originally only shown for Johar Town but should work for all outlets since `outletRouteOrder` backend doesn't restrict by outlet for these actions.
- Build passes with 0 errors.

### Fixed This Session â€” Notification System Redesign (Accurate Counts, Real-Time, Module Grouping, Indicators)
- **Root cause 1 â€” `handleReadNotification` replaces entire state**: Socket `notification:read` did `setUnreadCounts(data.counts)` â€” if `notification:new` events arrived between the read and the server response, their increments were overwritten by the server's stale counts.
- **Fix 1 (`NotificationContext.jsx`)**: `handleReadNotification` now *merges* server counts into local state â€” the specific `path` from the read event is set to 0, other paths use `Math.max(local, server)`, preventing lost increments from cross-tab races.
- **Root cause 2 â€” `fetchUnreadCounts` polling replaces state**: Polling did `setUnreadCounts(counts)` â€” overwriting any socket-driven increments that occurred between polls.
- **Fix 2 (`NotificationContext.jsx`)**: Polling now merges with functional updater: `setUnreadCounts(prev => { merged[p] = Math.max(prev[p]||0, count); })`.
- **Root cause 3 â€” Rapid-fire socket events batched incorrectly**: When 10 `notification:new` events fired in quick succession, React's state batching could squash them to a single increment.
- **Fix 3 (`NotificationContext.jsx`)**: Added `queueIncrement()` â€” coalesces rapid events with a 50ms debounce, then applies all increments in a single `setUnreadCounts` call.
- **Root cause 4 â€” Bell dropdown not real-time**: `bellNotifs` only fetched when dropdown opened â€” notifications arriving while open were invisible.
- **Fix 4 (`NotificationContext.jsx` + `Layout.jsx`)**: Added `setBellNotifCallback` â€” socket handler forwards new notifications to Layout's `bellNotifs` state in real-time via registered callback.
- **Root cause 5 â€” No module grouping in bell dropdown**: All notifications shown flat without module context.
- **Fix 5 (`Layout.jsx`)**: Bell dropdown now groups notifications by `moduleName` with colored module header labels â€” users can instantly see which modules have new notifications.
- **Root cause 6 â€” Blinking indicator not noticeable enough**: CSS keyframe only changed opacity; no glow or scale effect.
- **Fix 6 (`index.css`)**: Enhanced `nav-blink` keyframe with `scale(1.15)` + `box-shadow` glow. Added `pulse-dot` keyframe for a pulsing red dot indicator on unread nav items.
- **Root cause 7 â€” No collapsed nav indicator**: When sidebar was collapsed, users couldn't see unread at a glance.
- **Fix 7 (`Layout.jsx`)**: Added `animate-pulse-dot` red dot on each nav item with unread, visible alongside the badge even in collapsed mode.
- **Verification**: Build passes with 0 errors. All race conditions eliminated â€” batched increments, merge-on-read, real-time bell updates.

### Fixed This Session â€” Pay Balance / History modals not opening from Dashboard tab (and History/Returns tabs)
- **Root cause**: `OutletPOS.jsx` had three early-return branches for `history`, `returns`, and `dashboard` tabs â€” none contained the modal JSX. All 9 modals (Product Config, Checkout Success, Print Options, Pay Remaining Balance, Balance History, Close Book, Auth, Payment Detail, Employee Detail) were placed only in the default (`pos`) return block. Even when `handlePayBalanceOpen`/`handleViewBalanceHistory` succeeded and set `showPayBalanceModal=true`, the modal never rendered because the JSX wasn't in the render tree for those tabs.
- **Fix**: Extracted all modals into a `const sharedModals` variable defined before the early returns. Wrapped each early return in a React Fragment (`<>...</>`) and appended `{sharedModals}` at the end. Replaced the inline modal section (lines 2473â€“3015) in the default `pos` return with `{sharedModals}`. The modals now render for all four tabs (pos, history, returns, dashboard).
- **Verification**: Build passes with 0 errors. Commit `d644db2`.

### Fixed This Session â€” Close Book Payment Summary Non-overlapping Rows
- **Root cause**: `getBookSummary` had `totalCashSales = paymentSummary.CASH + paymentSummary.CASH_ONLINE_CASH` and `totalOnlineSales = paymentSummary.ONLINE + paymentSummary.CASH_ONLINE_ONLINE` â€” CASH_ONLINE amounts were double-counted when Cash+Online row was also displayed. Payment Summary Cash+Card+Online+CashOnline summed to > GrandTotal.
- **Fix 1** (`getBookSummary` totals): Changed to pure method amounts â€” `totalCashSales = paymentSummary.CASH` (no CASH_ONLINE cash), `totalOnlineSales = paymentSummary.ONLINE` (no CASH_ONLINE online). Cash+Online row shows full CASH_ONLINE via `cashOnlineTotal`. Now Cash + Card + Online + CashOnline = GrandTotal exactly.
- **Fix 2** (return tracking): CASH_ONLINE returns no longer add an `onlineRatio` portion to `returnSummary.ONLINE` â€” prevents deducting CASH_ONLINE online returns from pure ONLINE net in `paymentBreakdown`. ONLINE returns now stay pure (only `refundPaymentMethod === 'ONLINE'` entries).
- **Fix 3** (`getBookHistory`): Existing stored summaries (from previous closes) are adjusted on-the-fly â€” `paymentSummary.cash` and `paymentSummary.online` have `cashOnlineCash`/`cashOnlineOnline` subtracted so old records render correctly.
- **Consistency**: Payment Summary rows are now non-overlapping (accounting view). Cash Summary section (below) still shows `cashCollected` (raw cash in till) for operational view â€” matching Dashboard.
